import 'dotenv/config';
import { PrismaClient } from '@prisma/client';

// ðŸ›¡ï¸ Connessione diretta e blindata. Eliminato il Driver Adapter per superare la build Vercel
const prisma = new PrismaClient();

async function main() {
  const emailTest = "alessiopizzi258@gmail.com";

  // 1. Cerchiamo il Tenant creato dal seed
  const tenant = await prisma.tenant.findFirst({ where: { companyName: "Cosmea Studio Dentistico" } });
  if (!tenant) throw new Error("Tenant non trovato. Esegui prima il seed.");

  // 2. Creiamo o Attiviamo la Campagna
  const campaign = await prisma.campaign.upsert({
    where: { id: "campagna-test-1" }, // ID fisso per il test
    update: { status: "ACTIVE", dailyLimit: 50 },
    create: {
      id: "campagna-test-1",
      tenantId: tenant.id,
      name: "Campagna Riattivazione ARCH",
      status: "ACTIVE",
      dailyLimit: 50
    }
  });

  // 3. Reset del tuo Profilo per il primo invio
  await prisma.profile.update({
    where: { tenantId_email: { tenantId: tenant.id, email: emailTest } },
    data: { 
      lastContactedAt: null, // Forza l'invio immediato bypassando i filtri temporali
      segment: "Freddo"
    }
  });

  console.log("âœ… Campagna ATTIVATA.");
  console.log(`âœ… Profilo ${emailTest} pronto per il fuoco.`);
}

main().finally(() => prisma.$disconnect());

FILE: C:\Users\Alessio\Desktop\PROGETTI\nexus-engine\extract.js

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

const EXTS = ['.js', '.ts', '.jsx', '.tsx', '.json', '.php'];
const EXCLUDES = ['node_modules', '.git', '.next', 'build', 'dist', 'extract.js'];

function getAllFiles(dir, fileList = []) {
  const files = fs.readdirSync(dir);
  for (const file of files) {
    const filePath = path.join(dir, file);
    if (EXCLUDES.some(exclude => filePath.includes(exclude))) continue; // Salta brutalmente
    if (fs.statSync(filePath).isDirectory()) {
      getAllFiles(filePath, fileList);
    } else {
      if (EXTS.includes(path.extname(filePath))) {
        fileList.push(filePath);
      }
    }
  }
  return fileList;
}

const allFiles = getAllFiles(__dirname);
let output = '';

allFiles.forEach(file => {
  const content = fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8');
  output += `\n=== ${path.basename(file)} ===\n${content}\n`;
});

fs.writeFileSync('ai_context.txt', output);
console.log('Fatto! ai_context.txt generato istantaneamente.');

/**
 * @file public/nexus-shadow.js
 * @description Pixel di tracciamento passivo. InietterÃ  le sessioni fantasma sul ricevitore Edge.
 */

(function() {
    const CONFIG = {
        endpoint: "https://designlabstudio.it/api/pixel/shadow", // TUO DOMINIO
        tenantId: window.NEXUS_TENANT_ID,
        heartbeatMs: 5000 
    };

    let sessionData = {
        startTime: Date.now(),
        maxScroll: 0,
        activeTime: 0,
        lastPulse: Date.now()
    };

    const getFingerprint = () => {
        let fp = localStorage.getItem('_nxs_fp');
        if (!fp) {
            fp = btoa(navigator.userAgent + screen.width + screen.height + new Date().getTimezoneOffset());
            localStorage.setItem('_nxs_fp', fp);
        }
        return fp;
    };

    const trackActivity = () => {
        const now = Date.now();
        if (document.hasFocus()) {
            sessionData.activeTime += (now - sessionData.lastPulse);
        }
        sessionData.lastPulse = now;
        sessionData.maxScroll = Math.max(sessionData.maxScroll, window.scrollY);
    };

    setInterval(trackActivity, 1000);

    const sendPulse = (isFinal = false, capturedEmail = null) => {
        const payload = {
            tid: CONFIG.tenantId,
            fp: getFingerprint(),
            url: window.location.href,
            scroll: sessionData.maxScroll,
            duration: Math.floor(sessionData.activeTime / 1000),
            email: capturedEmail,
            isFinal: isFinal
        };

        if (navigator.sendBeacon) {
            navigator.sendBeacon(CONFIG.endpoint, JSON.stringify(payload));
        } else {
            fetch(CONFIG.endpoint, { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify(payload), keepalive: true });
        }
    };

    document.addEventListener('submit', function(e) {
        const emailInput = e.target.querySelector('input[type="email"]');
        if (emailInput && emailInput.value) {
            sendPulse(false, emailInput.value); 
        }
    });

    setInterval(() => sendPulse(false), CONFIG.heartbeatMs);
    window.addEventListener('beforeunload', () => sendPulse(true));
})();

FILE: C:\Users\Alessio\Desktop\PROGETTI\nexus-engine\src\proxy.ts
========================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import type { NextRequest } from 'next/server';
import { Ratelimit } from '@upstash/ratelimit';
import { Redis } from '@upstash/redis';

// 1. Inizializzazione della connessione Redis (Edge-compatible)
const redis = new Redis({
  url: process.env.UPSTASH_REDIS_REST_URL!,
  token: process.env.UPSTASH_REDIS_REST_TOKEN!,
});

// 2. Configurazione Sliding Window (5 req / 1 min)
const ratelimit = new Ratelimit({
  redis: redis,
  limiter: Ratelimit.slidingWindow(5, '1 m'),
  ephemeralCache: new Map(),
  analytics: false, // Disabilitato per ridurre latenza I/O
});

export async function proxy(request: NextRequest) {
  const { pathname } = request.nextUrl;

  // Applichiamo lo scudo solo sugli endpoint critici
  if (pathname.startsWith('/api/leads/ingest')) {
    
    // Identificazione del nodo (Vercel popola x-forwarded-for o ip nativo)
    const forwardedFor = request.headers.get('x-forwarded-for');
    const ip = forwardedFor ? forwardedFor.split(',')[0].trim() : '127.0.0.1';

    // --- DIFESA 1: RATE LIMITING ---
    const { success, limit, reset, remaining } = await ratelimit.limit(`ratelimit_${ip}`);
    
    if (!success) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: 'Too Many Requests', message: 'Quota superata. Riprova piÃ¹ tardi.' },
        { 
          status: 429, 
          headers: {
            'X-RateLimit-Limit': limit.toString(),
            'X-RateLimit-Remaining': remaining.toString(),
            'X-RateLimit-Reset': reset.toString(),
            'Retry-After': Math.ceil((reset - Date.now()) / 1000).toString()
          } 
        }
      );
    }

    // --- DIFESA 2: HONEYPOT (Ghost Block) ---
    if (request.method === 'POST') {
      try {
        // Cloniamo la request per non consumare lo stream destinato alla Route Handler
        const clonedReq = request.clone();
        const body = await clonedReq.json();
        
        // Ipotizziamo che 'website_url' o '_honeypot' siano i campi nascosti nel frontend
        if (body && (body.website_url || body._honeypot)) {
          // Risposta fantasma: il bot crede di aver avuto successo
          return NextResponse.json(
            { success: true, message: 'Operazione completata con successo.' }, 
            { status: 200 }
          );
        }
      } catch (e) {
        // Ignoriamo le eccezioni di parsing JSON a questo livello. 
        // Zod bloccherÃ  i payload corrotti direttamente nella Route Handler.
      }
    }
  }

  // --- DIFESA 3: HEADER DI SICUREZZA DI BASE ---
  const response = NextResponse.next();
  response.headers.set('X-Content-Type-Options', 'nosniff');
  response.headers.set('X-Frame-Options', 'DENY');
  response.headers.set('X-XSS-Protection', '1; mode=block');

  return response;
}

export const config = {
  matcher: ['/((?!_next/static|_next/image|favicon.ico).*)'],
};

/**
 * @file src/worker-ab-test.ts
 * @description
 * Worker BullMQ dedicato al lifecycle degli A/B Test. 
 * Valuta l'esito dei test dopo 48 ore misurando i tassi di apertura (open rate) 
 * tra due varianti ed elegge il vincitore aggiornando il database.
 */

import 'dotenv/config';
import { Worker, Job } from "bullmq";
import IORedis from "ioredis";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";

const connection = new IORedis(process.env.REDIS_URL!, { tls: { rejectUnauthorized: false } });

const abTestWorker = new Worker(
  "ab-test-evaluator",
  async (job: Job) => {
    const { campaignId, tenantId, variantAId, variantBId } = job.data;
    console.log(`[A/B TEST] Valutazione Campagna ${campaignId} a 48h...`);

    // 1. Estrazione metriche di Apertura
    const statsA = await prisma.automationLog.count({
      where: { campaignId, variantId: variantAId, status: "OPENED" }
    });
    
    const statsB = await prisma.automationLog.count({
      where: { campaignId, variantId: variantBId, status: "OPENED" }
    });

    const totalOpens = statsA + statsB;

    if (totalOpens === 0) {
      console.log("[A/B TEST] Nessun dato sufficiente. Mantengo Variante A per default.");
      return;
    }

    // 2. Calcolo Delta
    const winRateA = (statsA / totalOpens) * 100;
    const winRateB = (statsB / totalOpens) * 100;
    const delta = Math.abs(winRateA - winRateB);

    let winnerId = variantAId; // Fallback di sicurezza a Variante A

    // Se la differenza Ã¨ significativa (> 2%), si dichiara il vincitore
    if (delta > 2.0) {
      winnerId = winRateA > winRateB ? variantAId : variantBId;
      console.log(`ðŸ† [A/B TEST] Vincitore identificato. Variante: ${winnerId === variantAId ? 'A' : 'B'} (Delta: ${delta.toFixed(2)}%)`);
    } else {
      console.log(`âš–ï¸ [A/B TEST] Pareggio tecnico (Delta: ${delta.toFixed(2)}%). Confermo Variante A.`);
    }

    // 3. Elettore del Master Subject
    await prisma.campaign.update({
      where: { id: campaignId },
      data: { winningVariantId: winnerId, status: "OPTIMIZED" }
    });

  },
  { connection }
);

abTestWorker.on('ready', () => console.log("ðŸ§ª [A/B Test Worker] ONLINE."));

FILE: C:\Users\Alessio\Desktop\PROGETTI\nexus-engine\src\worker-lead-processor.ts
========================================

/**
 * @file src/worker-lead-processor.ts
 * @description
 * Worker BullMQ per l'elaborazione asincrona dei Power Leads.
 * Genera l'OTP, aggiorna il record su PostgreSQL e gestisce lo stato di qualificazione.
 * Ottimizzato per l'esecuzione in background (PM2/Docker).
 */

import 'dotenv/config';
import { Worker, Job } from 'bullmq';
import IORedis from 'ioredis';
import { prisma } from '@/lib/prisma';
import { LeadStatus } from '@prisma/client';
import crypto from 'crypto';

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------
// 1. INIZIALIZZAZIONE CONNESSIONE REDIS (I/O OPTIMIZED)
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------
const connection = new IORedis(process.env.REDIS_URL!, {
  maxRetriesPerRequest: null,
  tls: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' ? { rejectUnauthorized: false } : undefined,
});

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------
// 2. FUNZIONI QUANTITATIVE E DI SUPPORTO
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------
/**
 * Genera un OTP crittograficamente sicuro a 6 cifre.
 */
function generateSecureOTP(): string {
  return crypto.randomInt(100000, 999999).toString();
}

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------
// 3. ISTANZIAZIONE DEL WORKER
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------
const leadWorker = new Worker(
  'lead-processing',
  async (job: Job) => {
    // Gestione retrocompatibilitÃ  nome variabile (leadId vs profileId)
    const targetId = job.data.profileId || job.data.leadId;
    const icpScore = job.data.icpScore ?? 0;

    console.log(`[WORKER] Inizio elaborazione Job ${job.id} per Lead/Profile: ${targetId}`);

    if (!targetId) {
      throw new Error('Identificativo Lead mancante nel payload del Job.');
    }

    try {
      // 3A. Verifica esistenza record
      const profile = await prisma.profile.findUnique({
        where: { id: targetId },
      });

      if (!profile) {
        throw new Error(`Record Profile [${targetId}] non trovato nel database.`);
      }

      // 3B. Generazione OTP e definizione scadenza (10 minuti)
      const otpCode = generateSecureOTP();
      const expiresAt = new Date(Date.now() + 10 * 60000);

      // 3C. Transazione atomica: Salvataggio OTP + Aggiornamento Stato
      await prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
        // Scrittura del token
        await tx.otpToken.create({
          data: {
            token: otpCode,
            profileId: targetId,
            expiresAt: expiresAt,
          },
        });

        // Promozione di stato a QUALIFIED
        await tx.profile.update({
          where: { id: targetId },
          data: {
            status: LeadStatus.QUALIFIED,
            updatedAt: new Date(),
          },
        });
      });

      console.log(`âœ… [WORKER] Job ${job.id} completato. OTP generato e stato promosso a QUALIFIED.`);
      
      // Valore di ritorno memorizzato in Redis per tracciamento job
      return { success: true, targetId, otpCode, score: icpScore };

    } catch (error: unknown) {
      // 3D. Gestione parametrica dell'errore e declassamento stato
      const errorMessage = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Errore sconosciuto';
      console.error(`âŒ [WORKER] Fallimento Job ${job.id}:`, errorMessage);

      // Tentativo di fallback per evitare che il lead rimanga in un limbo
      try {
        await prisma.profile.update({
          where: { id: targetId },
          data: { status: LeadStatus.DA_VERIFICARE_MANUALMENTE },
        });
      } catch (fallbackError) {
        console.error(`âš ï¸ [WORKER] Impossibile aggiornare lo stato di fallback per ${targetId}`);
      }

      // Rilancia l'errore per permettere a BullMQ di gestire i tentativi (backoff)
      throw error;
    }
  },
  { 
    connection,
    concurrency: 5, // Parametrizzazione carico parallelo
  }
);

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------
// 4. EVENT LISTENERS & GRACEFUL SHUTDOWN (DEMONIZZAZIONE)
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------
leadWorker.on('ready', () => console.log('âš™ï¸ [Lead Processor Worker] ONLINE e in ascolto.'));
leadWorker.on('failed', (job, err) => console.error(`ðŸš¨ [Lead Processor Worker] Job ${job?.id} fallito definitivamente:`, err.message));

// Chiusura controllata per impedire la perdita di job durante il riavvio del demone
const gracefulShutdown = async (signal: string) => {
  console.log(`\n[${signal}] Avvio procedura di Graceful Shutdown per il Worker...`);
  await leadWorker.close();
  await prisma.$disconnect();
  connection.disconnect();
  console.log('Worker terminato in sicurezza. Uscita.');
  process.exit(0);
};

process.on('SIGINT', () => gracefulShutdown('SIGINT'));
process.on('SIGTERM', () => gracefulShutdown('SIGTERM'));

import type { Metadata } from "next";
import { Geist, Geist_Mono } from "next/font/google";
import "./globals.css";

const geistSans = Geist({
  variable: "--font-geist-sans",
  subsets: ["latin"],
});

const geistMono = Geist_Mono({
  variable: "--font-geist-mono",
  subsets: ["latin"],
});

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: "Create Next App",
  description: "Generated by create next app",
};

export default function RootLayout({
  children,
}: Readonly<{
  children: React.ReactNode;
}>) {

"use server";

import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";

export async function loginUser(email: string, license: string) {
  if (!email || !license) {
    return { error: "Compilare tutti i campi." };
  }

  // 1. Verifica nel Database
  const tenant = await prisma.tenant.findFirst({
    where: {
      adminEmail: email,
      apiKey: license
    }
  });

  if (!tenant) {
    return { error: "Credenziali non valide o licenza revocata." };
  }

  // 2. Generazione del Cookie di Sessione (Next.js 15 richiede await)
  const cookieStore = await cookies();
  cookieStore.set("nexus_session", tenant.id, {
    httpOnly: true,
    secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === "production",
    sameSite: "lax",
    path: "/",
    maxAge: 60 * 60 * 24 * 7 // Scade tra 7 giorni
  });

  return { success: true };
}

export async function logoutUser() {
  const cookieStore = await cookies();
  cookieStore.delete("nexus_session");
}

"use server";

import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { Resend } from "resend";
import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";

const resend = new Resend(process.env.RESEND_API_KEY);

export async function approveAndSendEmail(
  logId: string,
  finalHtml: string,
  finalSubject: string,
  tenantId: string,
  senderEmail: string
) {
  try {
    // 1. Recupera i dati del lead dal log
    const draft = await prisma.automationLog.findUnique({
      where: { id: logId }
    });

    if (!draft || draft.status !== "PENDING_APPROVAL") {
      return { success: false, error: "Bozza inesistente o giÃ  processata." };
    }

    // 2. Sparo con Resend
    const { data, error } = await resend.emails.send({
      from: `Design Lab Studio <alessio@designlabstudio.it>`, // Inserisci il TUO dominio verificato
      to: [draft.emailAddress],
      subject: finalSubject,
      html: finalHtml || "",
    });

    if (error) {
      console.error("Resend Error:", error);
      return { success: false, error: error.message };
    }

    // 3. Aggiorna DB (Sposta da Quarantena a Inviato)
    await prisma.$transaction([
      prisma.automationLog.update({
        where: { id: logId },
        data: { 
          status: "SENT", 
          errorMessage: null, 
          subject: finalSubject 
        }
      }),
      prisma.profile.update({
        where: { id: draft.profileId },
        data: { 
          lastContactedAt: new Date(),
          contactCount: { increment: 1 }
        }
      })
    ]);

    // Ricarica la pagina della dashboard in automatico
    revalidatePath("/dashboard/hitl");

    return { success: true, emailId: data?.id };

  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error("Errore critico in approvazione:", error);
    return { success: false, error: error.message };
  }
}

export async function rejectDraft(logId: string, tenantId: string) {
  try {
    // Elimina fisicamente la bozza o cambiane lo stato
    await prisma.automationLog.delete({
      where: { id: logId }
    });
    
    revalidatePath("/dashboard/hitl");
    return { success: true };
  } catch (error: any) {
    return { success: false, error: error.message };
  }
}

"use server";

import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";

export async function addWarmupAccount(formData: FormData) {
  const cookieStore = await cookies();
  const tenantId = cookieStore.get("nexus_session")?.value;
  
  if (!tenantId) return { error: "Violazione di sicurezza: Accesso negato." };

  const email = formData.get("email") as string;
  const appPassword = formData.get("appPassword") as string;
  const provider = formData.get("provider") as string; // "GMAIL" o "CUSTOM"
  
  const smtpHost = formData.get("smtpHost") as string;
  const smtpPort = parseInt(formData.get("smtpPort") as string) || 465;
  const imapHost = formData.get("imapHost") as string;
  const imapPort = parseInt(formData.get("imapPort") as string) || 993;

  if (!email || !appPassword) return { error: "Parametri incompleti. Inserire Email e Password." };

  if (provider === "CUSTOM" && (!smtpHost || !imapHost)) {
    return { error: "Per i server esterni sono necessari i parametri SMTP e IMAP." };
  }

  try {
    await prisma.gmailSender.create({
      data: {
        email,
        appPassword,
        provider,
        smtpHost: provider === "CUSTOM" ? smtpHost : "smtp.gmail.com",
        smtpPort: provider === "CUSTOM" ? smtpPort : 465,
        imapHost: provider === "CUSTOM" ? imapHost : "imap.gmail.com",
        imapPort: provider === "CUSTOM" ? imapPort : 993,
        dailyLimit: 10,
        sentToday: 0,
        isActive: true
      }
    });

    revalidatePath("/dashboard/warmup");
    return { success: true };
    
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);
    return { error: "Account giÃ  presente o errore database." };
  }
}

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { RfmService } from "@/lib/services/rfmService";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";
export const revalidate = 0; // Disinnesco totale della cache

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const authHeader = req.headers.get("Authorization");
    const secret = process.env.CRON_SECRET || "Al3s6i024@"; 
    
    if (authHeader !== `Bearer ${secret}`) {
      console.warn("âš ï¸ [Orchestratore] Tentativo di accesso negato.");
      return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: "Non autorizzato" }, { status: 401 });
    }

    const body = await req.json();
    const { tenantId } = body;

    if (!tenantId) {
      return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: "Tenant ID mancante." }, { status: 400 });
    }

    console.log(`âš™ï¸ [Orchestratore] Avvio Ricalcolo RFM Massivo per Tenant: ${tenantId}`);

    const profiles = await prisma.profile.findMany({
      where: {
        tenantId,
        segment: { not: "Bounced_Inattivo" } 
      }
    });

    let updatedCount = 0;

    for (const profile of profiles) {
      let r = 0, f = 0, m = 0, totalScore = 0;
      
      // ðŸ›¡ï¸ BASTA ACROBAZIE TYPESCRIPT: Usiamo un tipo flessibile per non far crashare Vercel
      let segment: any = "Freddo";

      if (profile.contactCount === 0 && profile.totalRevenue === 0) {
        segment = "Freddo";
      } else {
        r = RfmService.calculateRecency(profile.lastActivityAt);
        f = RfmService.calculateFrequency(profile.contactCount);
        m = RfmService.calculateMonetary(profile.totalRevenue);
        totalScore = r + f + m;
        segment = RfmService.classifySegment(totalScore);
      }

      const archAngle = RfmService.pickAngle(segment, profile.email);

      await prisma.profile.update({
        where: { id: profile.id },
        data: { 
          recency: r, 
          frequency: f, 
          monetary: m, 
          segment, 
          arch_angle: archAngle 
        }
      });
      
      updatedCount++;
    }

    console.log(`âœ… [Orchestratore] Completato. Profili allineati: ${updatedCount}`);

    return NextResponse.json({ 
      success: true, 
      message: "Orchestrazione ARCH completata con successo.",
      updatedCount 
    });

  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error("âŒ [ORCHESTRATOR_ERROR]", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: error.message }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import OpenAI from 'openai';

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';
export const maxDuration = 10; // Limite hardware Vercel Hobby

const deepseek = new OpenAI({
  baseURL: 'https://api.deepseek.com',
  apiKey: process.env.DEEPSEEK_API_KEY,
});

const APP_URL = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL || "https://nexus-engine-v1.vercel.app";

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  const authHeader = req.headers.get('authorization');
  const expectedToken = `Bearer ${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_CRON_SECRET || "Al3s6i024@"}`;

  if (authHeader !== expectedToken) {
    return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Non autorizzato" }, { status: 401 });
  }

  try {
    // 1A. Estrazione degli ID giÃ  in coda HITL (per non processarli due volte)
    const pendingLogs = await prisma.automationLog.findMany({
      where: { status: "PENDING_APPROVAL" },
      select: { profileId: true }
    });
    const lockedProfileIds = pendingLogs.map(log => log.profileId);

    // 1B. Pesca 2 lead "vergini" ignorando quelli giÃ  in Quarantena
    const leads = await prisma.profile.findMany({
      where: { 
        lastContactedAt: null, 
        email: { not: "" },
        id: { notIn: lockedProfileIds } // Esclusione matematica chirurgica
      },
      take: 2,
      orderBy: { createdAt: 'asc' }
    });

    if (leads.length === 0) {
      return NextResponse.json({ success: true, message: "Nessun lead disponibile per la generazione." });
    }

    let processedCount = 0;

    // 2. Generazione Sincrona con DeepSeek
    for (const profile of leads) {
      console.log(`ðŸ§  Ingegnerizzazione HITL per: ${profile.email}`);

      const systemPrompt = `Sei un Copywriter B2B di altissimo livello per "Design Lab Studio". Il tuo obiettivo Ã¨ scrivere una cold email a un target C-Level (imprenditori/direttori) in Italia.
Regole inviolabili:
1. ProfessionalitÃ  Estrema: Inizia sempre con "Buongiorno [Nome]" o "Gentile [Nome]".
2. Tono: Autorevole, educato, consulenziale. Nessuna arroganza, niente toni da venditore aggressivo. Devi sembrare un professionista serio che ha notato un'inefficienza reale.
3. Struttura in 3 frasi: (A) Contesto rapido del perchÃ© scrivi, (B) Il problema specifico o l'opportunitÃ  non sfruttata, (C) Una Call to Action morbida e non invadente.
4. Sintesi: Massimo 60-80 parole. 
5. Nessuna vendita: Non stai vendendo un servizio, stai proponendo uno scambio di idee su un potenziale collo di bottiglia.
6. Restituisci SOLO il testo pulito, usa <br><br> per andare a capo. Nessun tag html o markdown.`;

      const userPrompt = `Scrivi il testo per: ${profile.firstName || 'Responsabile'}.
L'angolo strategico Ã¨: ${profile.arch_angle || 'Analisi dei tassi di conversione bloccati da un ecosistema visivo non performante'}.
Suggerisci delicatamente che un check sul loro attuale posizionamento visivo potrebbe sbloccare margini inespressi. Chiudi chiedendo se sono aperti a una breve chiacchierata esplorativa la prossima settimana.`;

      const completion = await deepseek.chat.completions.create({
        messages: [{ role: "system", content: systemPrompt }, { role: "user", content: userPrompt }],
        model: "deepseek-chat",
        temperature: 0.35,
        max_tokens: 150,
      });

      let generatedHtml = completion.choices[0].message.content?.replace(/```html/gi, '').replace(/```/gi, '').replace(/^"|"$/g, '').trim() || "Errore AI.";

      // Iniezione tracciamento invisibile
      const infectedBody = generatedHtml.replace(
        /href=["'](https?:\/\/(www\.)?[^"']*)["']/gi, 
        (match, url) => `href="${APP_URL}/api/tracking?url=${encodeURIComponent(url)}&uid=${profile.id}&tid=${profile.tenantId}"`
      );

      const finalHtml = `
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="it">
<head><meta charset="UTF-8"></head>
<body style="font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 15px; color: #111111; line-height: 1.5; max-width: 600px; margin: 0;">
  <div>${infectedBody}</div>
  <img src="${APP_URL}/api/tracking/open?uid=${profile.id}&tid=${profile.tenantId}" width="1" height="1" style="display:none;" alt="" />
</body>
</html>`.trim();

      const subjectCompletion = await deepseek.chat.completions.create({
        messages: [
          { role: "system", content: "Scrivi un oggetto email interna. Max 3 parole, tutto minuscolo, zero punteggiatura." },
          { role: "user", content: `Testo: ${generatedHtml}` }
        ],
        model: "deepseek-chat",
        temperature: 0.4,
        max_tokens: 20
      });

      const generatedSubject = subjectCompletion.choices[0].message.content?.replace(/['"â€œâ€]/g, '').toLowerCase().trim() || "quick check";

      // 3. Transazione di salvataggio nel database
      await prisma.$transaction([
        prisma.automationLog.create({
          data: {
            tenantId: profile.tenantId,
            profileId: profile.id,
            emailAddress: profile.email,
            status: "PENDING_APPROVAL", 
            subject: generatedSubject,
            errorMessage: finalHtml, 
          }
        }),
        prisma.profile.update({
          where: { id: profile.id },
          data: { lastActivityAt: new Date() } // Sostituito lo "status" con un aggiornamento del timestamp di attivitÃ 
        })
      ]);

      processedCount++;
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, message: `Generazione completata. ${processedCount} bozze pronte in Quarantena.` });

  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error("âŒ Errore Daily Fire:", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: error.message }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  const authHeader = req.headers.get('authorization');
  const expectedToken = `Bearer ${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_CRON_SECRET || "Al3s6i024@"}`;

  if (authHeader !== expectedToken) {
    return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Non autorizzato" }, { status: 401 });
  }

  console.log("âš™ï¸ [Decay Engine] Avvio ciclo di decadimento punteggi passivi...");

  try {
    const DECAY_RATE = 10;
    const sevenDaysAgo = new Date(Date.now() - 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);

    // Seleziona profili inattivi da > 7 giorni e che NON hanno subito decadimento negli ultimi 7 giorni
    const targets = await prisma.profile.findMany({
      where: {
        lastActivityAt: { lt: sevenDaysAgo },
        passiveScore: { gt: 0 },
        OR: [
          { lastDecayedAt: null },
          { lastDecayedAt: { lt: sevenDaysAgo } }
        ]
      },
      select: { id: true, passiveScore: true }
    });

    if (targets.length === 0) {
      return NextResponse.json({ success: true, message: "Nessun decadimento necessario oggi." });
    }

    let decayedCount = 0;

    // Iniezione del tipo esplicito per bypassare il blocco di TypeScript
    const operations = targets.map((profile: { id: string; passiveScore: number }) => {
      const newScore = Math.max(0, profile.passiveScore - DECAY_RATE);
      
      return prisma.profile.update({
        where: { id: profile.id },
        data: {
          passiveScore: newScore,
          lastDecayedAt: new Date()
        }
      });
    });

    await prisma.$transaction(operations);
    decayedCount = operations.length;

    console.log(`âœ… [Decay Engine] Penalizzati ${decayedCount} profili per inattivitÃ .`);

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, decayedCount, message: "Decadimento completato." });

  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error("âŒ Errore Decay Engine:", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: error.message }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { prisma } from '@/lib/prisma';

// ðŸ›¡ï¸ BLINDA LA BUILD: Impedisce a Next.js di eseguire il cron durante la compilazione
export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

export async function GET() {
  console.log("ðŸ”„ [CRON] Inizio Warmup Protocol V2 (10 -> 100)...");

  try {
    const senders = await prisma.gmailSender.findMany({ where: { isActive: true } });

    for (const sender of senders) {
      let newLimit = sender.dailyLimit;

      // Se ieri ha lavorato (ha inviato piÃ¹ del 50% del suo limite), premiamolo con +10
      if (sender.sentToday >= Math.floor(sender.dailyLimit / 2)) {
        newLimit = Math.min(sender.dailyLimit + 10, 100); // Mai oltre i 100
      }

      await prisma.gmailSender.update({
        where: { id: sender.id },
        data: {
          sentToday: 0, // Azzera il contatore giornaliero
          dailyLimit: newLimit,
          quarantineUntil: null // Se era in quarantena, liberalo
        }
      });
      console.log(`ðŸ“ˆ [Warmup] ${sender.email}: Limite portato a ${newLimit}/giorno.`);
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ message: "Warmup completato con successo." }, { status: 200 });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Errore Cron Warmup:", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Fallimento CRON" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { Resend } from 'resend';

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';
const resend = new Resend(process.env.RESEND_API_KEY);

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const { logId } = await req.json();

    // 1. Recuperiamo la bozza dal database
    const draft = await prisma.automationLog.findUnique({
      where: { id: logId }
    });

    if (!draft || draft.status !== "PENDING_APPROVAL") {
      return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Bozza non trovata o giÃ  processata." }, { status: 400 });
    }

    // 2. Invio fulmineo con Resend
    const { data, error } = await resend.emails.send({
      from: `Design Lab Studio <alessio@designlabstudio.it>`, 
      to: [draft.emailAddress],
      subject: draft.subject || "Aggiornamento",
      html: draft.errorMessage || "", 
    });

    if (error) throw new Error(`Resend Error: ${error.message}`);

    // 3. Aggiorniamo il DB: Email inviata
    await prisma.$transaction([
      prisma.automationLog.update({
        where: { id: logId },
        data: { status: "SENT", errorMessage: null } // Puliamo l'errorMessage e mettiamo SENT
      }),
      prisma.profile.update({
        where: { id: draft.profileId },
        data: { lastContactedAt: new Date() }
      })
    ]);

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, message: "Email approvata e spedita.", emailId: data?.id });

  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error("âŒ Errore Approvazione:", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: error.message }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

export async function DELETE(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    // 1. Scudo di sicurezza: operazione consentita solo con API Key autorizzata
    const apiKey = req.headers.get("x-nexus-api-key");
    if (!apiKey) return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Accesso negato." }, { status: 401 });

    const tenant = await prisma.tenant.findFirst({ where: { apiKey: apiKey } });
    if (!tenant) return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Credenziali non valide." }, { status: 403 });

    const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
    const profileId = searchParams.get("id");

    if (!profileId) {
      return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "ID Profilo mancante." }, { status: 400 });
    }

    // 2. Annientamento a cascata (Transazione Atomica)
    // Ãˆ essenziale eliminare prima i record relazionali per evitare violazioni dei vincoli di chiave esterna.
    await prisma.$transaction([
      prisma.otpToken.deleteMany({ where: { profileId } }),
      prisma.webActivity.deleteMany({ where: { profileId } }),
      prisma.trackingEvent.deleteMany({ where: { profileId } }),
      prisma.automationLog.deleteMany({ where: { profileId } }),
      prisma.profile.delete({ where: { id: profileId, tenantId: tenant.id } })
    ]);

    console.log(`ðŸ—‘ï¸ [GDPR Compliance] Epurazione completata con successo per il profilo: ${profileId}`);

    return NextResponse.json({ 
      success: true, 
      message: "Oblio eseguito. Tutti i dati operativi e anagrafici sono stati distrutti." 
    }, { status: 200 });

  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error("âŒ [GDPR Erase Error]:", error.message);
    return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Impossibile completare l'operazione di cancellazione." }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


========================================
FILE: C:\Users\Alessio\Desktop\PROGETTI\nexus-engine\src\app\api\leads\ingest\route.ts
========================================

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { z } from "zod";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { LeadStatus } from "@prisma/client";
import { Queue } from "bullmq";
import IORedis from "ioredis";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------
// 1. INIZIALIZZAZIONE CODE (BULLMQ + REDIS)
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------
// Il riutilizzo della connessione evita memory leak su istanze serverless
const connection = new IORedis(process.env.REDIS_URL!, {
  maxRetriesPerRequest: null,
  tls: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' ? { rejectUnauthorized: false } : undefined
});
const leadQueue = new Queue('lead-processing', { connection });

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------
// 2. SCHEMA DI VALIDAZIONE BLINDATO (ZOD)
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------
const ingestSchema = z.object({
  firstName: z.string().min(2, "Nome troppo corto").optional(),
  lastName: z.string().min(2, "Cognome troppo corto").optional(),
  email: z.string().email("Email non valida"),
  phone: z.string().optional(),
  urgency: z.enum(["HIGH", "MEDIUM", "LOW"]),
  role: z.enum(["OWNER", "MANAGER", "DIRECTOR", "C_LEVEL", "EMPLOYEE", "OTHER"]),
  budget: z.number().nullable().optional(),
  problemDescription: z.string().optional(),
});

type IngestData = z.infer<typeof ingestSchema>;

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------
// 3. SETACCIO MATEMATICO (ICP SCORING ENGINE)
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------
function calculateICPScore(data: IngestData): number {
  let score = 0;

  // Modulatore Urgenza (Max 40)
  if (data.urgency === "HIGH") score += 40;
  else if (data.urgency === "MEDIUM") score += 20;

  // Modulatore Potere Decisionale (Max 30)
  if (["OWNER", "C_LEVEL", "DIRECTOR"].includes(data.role)) score += 30;
  else if (data.role === "MANAGER") score += 15;

  // Modulatore CapacitÃ  di Spesa (Max 30)
  if (data.budget && data.budget > 0) score += 30;

  // Cap massimo per sicurezza costrutto
  return Math.min(score, 100);
}

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------
// 4. ROUTE HANDLER POST
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------
export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    // A. Autenticazione Server-Side (Nessun tenantId nel body, blindato tramite API Key)
    const apiKey = req.headers.get("x-rls-api-key");
    if (!apiKey) return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Non autorizzato" }, { status: 401 });

    const tenant = await prisma.tenant.findUnique({ where: { apiKey } });
    if (!tenant) return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Credenziali Tenant non valide" }, { status: 403 });

    // B. Parsing e Validazione Strict
    const body = await req.json();
    const parsed = ingestSchema.safeParse(body);
    
    if (!parsed.success) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, message: "Payload non conforme ai requisiti RLS", errors: parsed.error.format() },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const data = parsed.data;

    // C. Calcolo Algoritmico
    const icpScore = calculateICPScore(data);
    
    // Condizionale di stato: scartiamo (NURTURING) chi non supera la soglia logica minima
    const targetStatus = icpScore >= 50 ? LeadStatus.PENDING_OTP : LeadStatus.NURTURING;

    // D. Persistenza Dati (Upsert per evitare duplicati logici)
    const profile = await prisma.profile.upsert({
      where: { tenantId_email: { tenantId: tenant.id, email: data.email } },
      update: {
        contactCount: { increment: 1 },
        lastActivityAt: new Date(),
        urgency: data.urgency,
        role: data.role,
        budget: data.budget,
        problemDescription: data.problemDescription,
        icpScore: icpScore,
      },
      create: {
        tenantId: tenant.id,
        email: data.email,
        firstName: data.firstName || "",
        lastName: data.lastName || "",
        phone: data.phone || "",
        source: "RLS_Algorithmic_Ingestion",
        segment: "Freddo",
        status: targetStatus,
        urgency: data.urgency,
        role: data.role,
        budget: data.budget,
        problemDescription: data.problemDescription,
        icpScore: icpScore,
        isVerified: false,
        lastActivityAt: new Date(),
      }
    });

    // E. Esecuzione Asincrona (Offload su BullMQ)
    // Selezioniamo solo i "Power Leads" per l'elaborazione prioritaria
    if (icpScore > 70) {
      await leadQueue.add(
        "process-power-lead", 
        { profileId: profile.id, tenantId: tenant.id, icpScore },
        { 
          attempts: 3, 
          backoff: { type: "exponential", delay: 2000 },
          removeOnComplete: true 
        }
      );
      console.log(`ðŸš€ [RLS] Power Lead accodato su BullMQ. (Score: ${icpScore}, Email: ${data.email})`);
    } else {
      console.log(`âš–ï¸ [RLS] Lead standard intercettato. (Score: ${icpScore}, Status: ${targetStatus})`);
    }

    // F. Risposta istantanea (Latenza ottimizzata)
    return NextResponse.json({ 
      success: true, 
      profileId: profile.id,
      status: targetStatus,
      score: icpScore,
      message: "Lead intercettato e sottoposto a setaccio matematico."
    }, { status: 200 });

  } catch (error: unknown) {
    // Gestione errori controllata per evitare il collasso del processo
    if (error instanceof Error) {
      console.error("âŒ [RLS Ingestion FATAL ERROR]:", error.message);
    } else {
      console.error("âŒ [RLS Ingestion FATAL ERROR]: Unknown exception");
    }
    return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Errore interno del server" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

FILE: C:\Users\Alessio\Desktop\PROGETTI\nexus-engine\src\app\api\leads\verify-otp\route.ts
========================================

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { z } from "zod";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { RfmService } from "@/lib/services/rfmService";
import { LeadStatus } from "@prisma/client";
import { DeliveryService } from "@/lib/services/deliveryService";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

// 1. SCHEMA DI VALIDAZIONE STRICT (ZOD)
const verifySchema = z.object({
  profileId: z.string().min(1, "Profile ID obbligatorio"),
  token: z.string().length(6, "Il token deve essere di 6 cifre").regex(/^\d+$/, "Solo numeri"),
});

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    // A. Parsing e Validazione
    const body = await req.json();
    const parsed = verifySchema.safeParse(body);

    if (!parsed.success) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, message: "Parametri mancanti o non validi." }, 
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const { profileId, token } = parsed.data;

    // B. Ricerca e validazione temporale del Token
    const otpRecord = await prisma.otpToken.findFirst({
      where: {
        profileId: profileId,
        token: token,
        expiresAt: { gt: new Date() } // Accetta solo token nel futuro
      },
      include: {
        profile : true
      }
    });

    if (!otpRecord) {
      return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Codice OTP non valido o scaduto." }, { status: 401 });
    }

    // C. Sblocco del Profilo in transazione atomica
    await prisma.$transaction([
      prisma.profile.update({
        where: { id: profileId },
        data: { 
          isVerified: true, 
          status: LeadStatus.QUALIFIED // <-- Allineato all'infrastruttura RLS
        } 
      }),
      prisma.otpToken.deleteMany({
        where: { profileId: profileId } // Epurazione totale dei token per questo lead
      })
    ]);

    // D. Ricalcolo Scoring e Iniezione nel CRM (La tua logica originale)
    const syncResult = await RfmService.syncProfileScore(profileId);
    DeliveryService.sendLeadToTenant(profileId, otpRecord.profile.tenantId).catch(console.error);
    console.log(`âœ… [Livello 2] OTP confermato. Profilo ${profileId} armato e inserito nel segmento: ${syncResult?.newSegment}`);

    // E. Risposta al client
    return NextResponse.json({ 
      success: true, 
      message: "Verifica completata. Accesso al sistema sbloccato.",
      segment: syncResult?.newSegment
    }, { status: 200 });

  } catch (error: unknown) {
    const errorMessage = error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Errore sconosciuto";
    console.error("âŒ [OTP Fatal Error]:", errorMessage);
    return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Errore interno del server." }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { TrafficScoringService } from "@/lib/services/trafficScoringService";
import { RfmService } from "@/lib/services/rfmService";

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const body = await req.json();
    const { tid, fp, url, scroll, duration, email, isFinal } = body;
    
    let profile = null;
    if (email) {
        profile = await prisma.profile.findFirst({ where: { email, tenantId: tid } });
    } else {
        const lastWebActivity = await prisma.webActivity.findFirst({ 
            where: { fingerprint: fp, profileId: { not: null } }, 
            orderBy: { timestamp: 'desc' } 
        });
        if (lastWebActivity?.profileId) {
            profile = await prisma.profile.findUnique({ where: { id: lastWebActivity.profileId }});
        }
    }

    let activity = await prisma.webActivity.findFirst({
        where: { fingerprint: fp, url: url, timestamp: { gte: new Date(Date.now() - 1000 * 60 * 30) } } 
    });

    let newPoints = 0;
    let urlScoreAwarded = activity?.urlScoreAwarded || false;

    if (!urlScoreAwarded) {
        newPoints += TrafficScoringService.calculatePageScore(url);
        urlScoreAwarded = true;
    }

    const previousDuration = activity?.duration || 0;
    newPoints += TrafficScoringService.getDurationBonus(duration, previousDuration);

    if (activity) {
        await prisma.webActivity.update({
            where: { id: activity.id },
            data: { duration, scrollDepth: scroll, urlScoreAwarded }
        });
    } else {
        await prisma.webActivity.create({
            data: {
                profileId: profile?.id || null,
                fingerprint: fp,
                url: url,
                duration: duration,
                scrollDepth: scroll,
                urlScoreAwarded
            }
        });
    }

    if (profile && newPoints > 0) {
        await prisma.profile.update({
            where: { id: profile.id },
            data: { 
                passiveScore: { increment: newPoints },
                lastActivityAt: new Date() 
            }
        });

        // ðŸ‘‰ TRIGGER RICALCOLO GLOBALE
        await RfmService.syncProfileScore(profile.id);
        
        // Trigger Agguato per i Dormienti ad Alto Watch-Time
        if (profile.segment === "Dormiente" && duration > 30 && (url.includes('audit') || url.includes('libro'))) {
            await prisma.automationLog.create({
                data: {
                    tenantId: tid,
                    profileId: profile.id,
                    emailAddress: profile.email,
                    status: "SYSTEM_ALERT",
                    subject: "ðŸ”¥ LEAD DORMIENTE RISVEGLIATO",
                    errorMessage: `Ha letto l'offerta per ${duration}s. Richiede contatto immediato (Recency Effect).`
                }
            });
        }
    }

    return new NextResponse(JSON.stringify({ status: 'tracked', pointsAdded: newPoints }), {
      status: 200,
      headers: { 'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*', 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }
    });

  } catch (error) {
    console.error("[Shadow Pixel Error]", error);
    return new NextResponse(JSON.stringify({ error: "Pixel Error" }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*' } });
  }
}

/**
 * @file src/app/api/tenant/export/route.ts
 * @description
 * Esporta il database isolato del cliente in formato CSV.
 * Protetto da Basic Auth. Risponde alla richiesta di ownership dei dati.
 */

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  // Sicurezza Basic Auth (Identica a middleware)
  const authHeader = req.headers.get('authorization');
  const expectedAuth = `Basic ${btoa(`${process.env.ADMIN_USER}:${process.env.ADMIN_PASS}`)}`;
  
  if (authHeader !== expectedAuth) {
    return new NextResponse('Non autorizzato', { status: 401 });
  }

  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const tenantId = searchParams.get("tid");

  if (!tenantId) return new NextResponse("Tenant ID richiesto", { status: 400 });

  try {
    const profiles = await prisma.profile.findMany({
      where: { tenantId },
      select: { email: true, firstName: true, lastName: true, segment: true, totalRevenue: true, lastActivityAt: true }
    });

    // Costruzione manuale CSV ad alte prestazioni
    const headers = "Email,Nome,Cognome,Segmento_ARCH,Fatturato_Acquisito,Ultima_Attivita\n";
    const csvContent = profiles.map(p => 
      `${p.email},${p.firstName || ''},${p.lastName || ''},${p.segment},${p.totalRevenue || 0},${p.lastActivityAt?.toISOString() || ''}`
    ).join("\n");

    const fullCsv = headers + csvContent;

    return new NextResponse(fullCsv, {
      headers: {
        "Content-Type": "text/csv",
        "Content-Disposition": `attachment; filename="database_arch_${tenantId}.csv"`,
      }
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return new NextResponse("Errore di esportazione", { status: 500 });
  }
}

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { RfmService } from "@/lib/services/rfmService";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  
  const uid = searchParams.get("uid");
  const tid = searchParams.get("tid");
  const cid = searchParams.get("cid");
  const targetUrl = searchParams.get("url");

  if (!targetUrl) {
    return NextResponse.redirect(process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL || "https://designlabstudio.it", 302);
  }

  if (!uid || !tid) {
    console.warn(`âš ï¸ [Tracking] Click anonimo o parametri mancanti verso: ${targetUrl}`);
    return NextResponse.redirect(targetUrl, 302);
  }

  try {
    const [profile] = await Promise.all([
      prisma.profile.update({
        where: { id: uid },
        data: { 
          totalClicks: { increment: 1 },
          lastActivityAt: new Date() 
        },
        select: { totalClicks: true, email: true }
      }),
      prisma.trackingEvent.create({
        data: {
          type: "CLICK",
          url: targetUrl,
          profileId: uid,
          tenantId: tid,
          campaignId: cid || null,
        }
      })
    ]);

    // ðŸ‘‰ TRIGGER RICALCOLO GLOBALE
    const syncResult = await RfmService.syncProfileScore(uid);

    // TRIGGER COMPORTAMENTALE BASATO SUL SEGMENTO DINAMICO
    if (syncResult?.newSegment === "Caldo" || syncResult?.newSegment === "Urgente") {
      console.log(`ðŸ”¥ [Trigger] Il lead ${profile.email} ha raggiunto il segmento ${syncResult.newSegment}.`);
      
      await prisma.$transaction([
        prisma.automationLog.create({
          data: {
            tenantId: tid,
            profileId: uid,
            emailAddress: profile.email,
            status: "SYSTEM_ALERT",
            subject: `ðŸ”¥ TRIGGER: Target ${syncResult.newSegment}`,
            errorMessage: `Il lead ha accumulato ${syncResult.integratedScore} punti integrati. Massimo interesse rilevato. Contattare per chiusura.`,
            timestamp: new Date()
          }
        })
      ]);
    }

  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error(`âŒ [Tracking CRITICAL] Errore logica DB:`, error.message);
  }

  return NextResponse.redirect(targetUrl, 302);
}

      console.error("âŒ [Tracker Error] Impossibile registrare apertura:", error);
    }
  }

    status: 200,
    headers: {
      'Content-Type': 'image/gif',
      'Cache-Control': 'no-store, no-cache, must-revalidate, proxy-revalidate',
      'Pragma': 'no-cache',
      'Expires': '0',

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { prisma } from '@/lib/prisma';

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  // 1. Estrazione chirurgica dell'ID dalla query string dell'URL
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const profileId = searchParams.get('id');

  if (!profileId) {
    return new NextResponse('Identificativo mancante o corrotto.', { status: 400 });
  }

  try {
    // 2. Isolamento del Target: Spostamento nel segmento "Bounced_Inattivo"
    // Usiamo un update in isolamento per evitare di alterare altre metriche
    await prisma.profile.update({
      where: { id: profileId },
      data: { 
        segment: "Bounced_Inattivo",
        // Opzionale: azzeriamo le metriche future
        arch_angle: "UNSUBSCRIBED"
      }
    });

    // 3. (Opzionale ma letale) Logghiamo l'azione per le tue statistiche interne
    await prisma.automationLog.create({
      data: {
        tenantId: (await prisma.profile.findUnique({ where: { id: profileId } }))?.tenantId || "UNKNOWN",
        profileId: profileId,
        emailAddress: "HIDDEN_FOR_COMPLIANCE", // Nascondiamo per compliance
        subject: "USER_OPT_OUT",
        status: "BLOCKED" 
      }
    });

    // 4. Conferma visiva all'utente (Fredda e istituzionale)
    const htmlResponse = `
      <html>
        <body style="font-family: Arial, sans-serif; text-align: center; padding: 50px; background-color: #f9f9f9;">
          <h2 style="color: #333;">Aggiornamento Registri</h2>
          <p style="color: #666;">Il tuo indirizzo email Ã¨ stato rimosso con successo dai nostri sistemi.</p>
          <p style="color: #666;">Non riceverai ulteriori comunicazioni da Paolucci Advisory.</p>
        </body>
      </html>
    `;

    return new NextResponse(htmlResponse, { 
      status: 200, 
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'text/html' } 
    });

  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error("âŒ [UNSUBSCRIBE_ERROR] Fallimento aggiornamento database:", error);
    return new NextResponse('Errore durante l\'elaborazione della richiesta.', { status: 500 });
  }
}

"use server";

import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
import { RfmService } from "@/lib/services/rfmService";
import * as xlsx from "xlsx";

export async function addSingleLead(formData: FormData, tenantId: string) {
  const email = formData.get("email")?.toString().trim();
  const firstName = formData.get("firstName")?.toString().trim() || "";
  const lastName = formData.get("lastName")?.toString().trim() || "";
  const phone = formData.get("phone")?.toString().trim() || "";

  if (!email) return { error: "L'email Ã¨ obbligatoria." };

  const coldDate = new Date(Date.now() - 365 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);

  try {
    await prisma.profile.upsert({
      where: { tenantId_email: { tenantId, email } },
      update: { firstName, lastName, phone }, 
      create: {
        tenantId, email, firstName, lastName, phone,
        source: "DesignLab_Manual", segment: "Freddo",
        contactCount: 0, totalRevenue: 0, lastActivityAt: coldDate
      }
    });

    revalidatePath("/dashboard");
    return { success: true };
  } catch (error: any) {
    return { error: `Errore database: ${error.message}` };
  }
}

export async function importCsvLeads(formData: FormData, tenantId: string) {
  const file = formData.get("file") as File;
  if (!file) return { error: "Nessun file selezionato." };

  try {
    const arrayBuffer = await file.arrayBuffer();
    const buffer = Buffer.from(arrayBuffer);

    const workbook = xlsx.read(buffer, { type: "buffer" });
    const sheetName = workbook.SheetNames[0];
    const sheet = workbook.Sheets[sheetName];

    const rawData: any[] = xlsx.utils.sheet_to_json(sheet, { defval: "" });

    if (rawData.length === 0) return { error: "Il file Ã¨ vuoto o illeggibile." };

    let successCount = 0;
    const coldDate = new Date(Date.now() - 365 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);

    const ops = rawData.map(async (row) => {
      const keys = Object.keys(row);

      const emailKey = keys.find(k => {
        const l = k.toLowerCase().trim();
        return l.includes("email") || l.includes("e-mail") || l === "mail";
      });

      let email = emailKey ? String(row[emailKey]).trim() : null;

      if (!email) {
         const valueWithAt = Object.values(row).find(v => typeof v === 'string' && v.includes('@') && v.includes('.'));
         if (valueWithAt) email = String(valueWithAt).trim();
      }

      if (!email || !email.includes("@")) return;

      const firstNameKey = keys.find(k => k.toLowerCase().includes("first") || k.toLowerCase().includes("nome"));
      const lastNameKey = keys.find(k => k.toLowerCase().includes("last") || k.toLowerCase().includes("cognome"));
      const phoneKey = keys.find(k => k.toLowerCase().includes("phone") || k.toLowerCase().includes("telefono"));

      const firstName = firstNameKey ? String(row[firstNameKey]).trim() : "";
      const lastName = lastNameKey ? String(row[lastNameKey]).trim() : "";
      const phone = phoneKey ? String(row[phoneKey]).trim() : "";

      await prisma.profile.upsert({
        where: { tenantId_email: { tenantId, email } },
        update: {}, 
        create: {
          tenantId,
          email,
          firstName,
          lastName,
          phone,
          source: "DesignLab_Data_Import",
          segment: "Freddo",
          contactCount: 0,
          totalRevenue: 0,
          lastActivityAt: coldDate
        }
      });
      successCount++;
    });

    await Promise.all(ops);
    revalidatePath("/dashboard");
    return { success: true, count: successCount };

  } catch (error: any) {
    return { error: `Errore critico motore ETL: ${error.message}` };
  }
}

export async function syncRfmDatabase(tenantId: string) {
  try {
    const profiles = await prisma.profile.findMany({
      where: {
        tenantId,
        segment: { not: "Bounced_Inattivo" }
      }
    });

    let updatedCount = 0;

    for (const profile of profiles) {
      let r = 0, f = 0, m = 0, totalScore = 0;
      let segment: Parameters<typeof RfmService.pickAngle>[0] = "Freddo";

      if (profile.contactCount === 0 && profile.totalRevenue === 0) {
        segment = "Freddo";
      } else {
        r = RfmService.calculateRecency(profile.lastActivityAt);
        f = RfmService.calculateFrequency(profile.contactCount);
        m = RfmService.calculateMonetary(profile.totalRevenue);
        totalScore = r + f + m;
        segment = RfmService.classifySegment(totalScore) as typeof segment;
      }

      const archAngle = RfmService.pickAngle(segment, profile.email);

      await prisma.profile.update({
        where: { id: profile.id },
        data: { recency: r, frequency: f, monetary: m, segment, arch_angle: archAngle }
      });
      
      updatedCount++;
    }

    revalidatePath("/dashboard");
    return { success: true, updatedCount };
  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error("âŒ Errore Server Action Orchestratore:", error);
    return { error: error.message };
  }
}

import Sidebar from "./Sidebar";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";

export default async function DashboardLayout({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const cookieStore = await cookies();
  const sessionId = cookieStore.get("nexus_session")?.value;

  if (!sessionId) redirect("/");

  const tenant = await prisma.tenant.findUnique({ where: { id: sessionId } });
  if (!tenant) redirect("/");

  return (
    <div className="flex h-screen bg-slate-50 text-slate-900 font-sans selection:bg-emerald-500/20 overflow-hidden">
      <Sidebar />
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col h-screen overflow-hidden">
        <header className="h-16 border-b border-slate-200 bg-white px-8 flex justify-end items-center z-50 shrink-0">
          <div className="text-[10px] tracking-widest text-slate-500 uppercase font-medium">
            Licenza attiva: <span className="text-emerald-600 font-bold ml-1">{tenant.companyName}</span>
          </div>
        </header>
        <main className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto custom-scrollbar p-8">
          <div className="max-w-[1400px] mx-auto">
            {children}
          </div>
        </main>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useState, useRef } from "react";
import { addSingleLead, importCsvLeads } from "./actions";

export default function LeadManager({ tenantId }: { tenantId: string }) {
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState<{ text: string; type: "error" | "success" } | null>(null);
  const formRef = useRef<HTMLFormElement>(null);
  const fileRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  async function handleSingleSubmit(e: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    setMessage(null);

    const formData = new FormData(e.currentTarget);
    const result = await addSingleLead(formData, tenantId);

    if (result.error) setMessage({ text: result.error, type: "error" });
    else {
      setMessage({ text: "Database aggiornato (Metodo Redi).", type: "success" });
      formRef.current?.reset();
    }
    setLoading(false);
  }

  async function handleCsvUpload(e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;

    setLoading(true);
    setMessage(null);

    const formData = new FormData();
    formData.append("file", file);

    const result = await importCsvLeads(formData, tenantId);

    if (result.error) setMessage({ text: result.error, type: "error" });
    else setMessage({ text: `${result.count} Lead iniettati nel sistema.`, type: "success" });
    
    if (fileRef.current) fileRef.current.value = "";
    setLoading(false);
  }

  return (
    <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-8 h-full shadow-sm">
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-8">
        <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-800 uppercase tracking-widest">Inserimento Strategico</h3>
        <label className={`cursor-pointer text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest px-4 py-2 border rounded-md transition-colors ${loading ? 'opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed border-slate-200 text-slate-400' : 'border-emerald-200 text-emerald-600 bg-emerald-50 hover:bg-emerald-100'}`}>
          {loading ? "Elaborazione..." : "Importa CSV"}
          <input type="file" accept=".csv, .xlsx, .xls" className="hidden" ref={fileRef} onChange={handleCsvUpload} disabled={loading} />
        </label>
      </div>

      <form ref={formRef} onSubmit={handleSingleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
        <input type="email" name="email" required placeholder="Email Cliente *" className="w-full bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-slate-900 px-4 py-3 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-emerald-500 rounded-md placeholder-slate-400 transition-all" />
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
          <input type="text" name="firstName" placeholder="Nome" className="w-full bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-slate-900 px-4 py-3 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-emerald-500 rounded-md placeholder-slate-400 transition-all" />
          <input type="text" name="lastName" placeholder="Cognome" className="w-full bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-slate-900 px-4 py-3 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-emerald-500 rounded-md placeholder-slate-400 transition-all" />
        </div>
        <input type="text" name="phone" placeholder="Telefono" className="w-full bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-slate-900 px-4 py-3 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-emerald-500 rounded-md placeholder-slate-400 transition-all" />
        
        <button type="submit" disabled={loading} className="w-full bg-emerald-500 text-white font-bold text-[11px] tracking-widest uppercase py-3.5 mt-4 rounded-md hover:bg-emerald-600 transition-colors disabled:opacity-50 shadow-sm shadow-emerald-500/20">
          Inietta nel Sistema
        </button>
      </form>

      {message && (
        <div className={`mt-6 p-4 text-xs font-bold tracking-widest uppercase rounded-md border ${message.type === 'success' ? 'bg-emerald-50 border-emerald-200 text-emerald-600' : 'bg-red-50 border-red-200 text-red-600'}`}>
          {message.text}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import PriorityList from "./PriorityList";
import { LeadStatus } from "@prisma/client";

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

interface DashboardPageProps {
  searchParams: Promise<{ [key: string]: string | string[] | undefined }>;
}

export default async function DashboardOverview({ searchParams }: DashboardPageProps) {
  const resolvedParams = await searchParams;
  const currentFilter = (resolvedParams.filter as string) || "ALL";

  const tenant = await prisma.tenant.findFirst();
  if (!tenant) return <div className="p-8 text-slate-500 text-xs uppercase tracking-widest font-medium">Nessuna Licenza Attiva.</div>;

  // 1. Estrazione massiva ottimizzata con indici relazionali
  const profiles = await prisma.profile.findMany({
    where: { tenantId: tenant.id },
    orderBy: { lastActivityAt: 'desc' }
  });

  // Data di riferimento per calcoli temporali (es. ultimi 7 giorni = freschezza critica)
  const now = new Date().getTime();
  const SEVEN_DAYS_MS = 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;

  // 2. EQUAZIONI DI FILTRAGGIO MULTIDIMENSIONALE
  const processedProfiles = profiles.map(p => {
    const lastActivityTime = p.lastActivityAt ? new Date(p.lastActivityAt).getTime() : new Date(p.createdAt).getTime();
    const isRecent = (now - lastActivityTime) <= SEVEN_DAYS_MS;
    
    // Equazione di valore ponderato (Matematica + Temporale)
    // Un lead Ã¨ "Power" se Ã¨ qualificato, ha un buon score ICP ed Ã¨ attivo recentemente o ha budget alto
    const isPowerLead = p.status === LeadStatus.QUALIFIED && p.icpScore >= 60 && (p.budget !== null && p.budget > 0);
    const isUrgent = p.urgency === "HIGH" && p.status === LeadStatus.QUALIFIED;
    const isStale = !isRecent && p.status === LeadStatus.NURTURING;

    return {
      ...p,
      _metrics: {
        isRecent,
        isPowerLead,
        isUrgent,
        isStale
      }
    };
  });

  // 3. CALCOLO KPI GLOBALI (Basati su equazioni matematiche e finanziarie reali)
  const qualifiedLeads = processedProfiles.filter(p => p.status === LeadStatus.QUALIFIED);
  const powerLeadsCount = processedProfiles.filter(p => p._metrics.isPowerLead).length;
  const urgentCount = processedProfiles.filter(p => p._metrics.isUrgent).length;
  const pendingCount = processedProfiles.filter(p => p.status === LeadStatus.PENDING_OTP).length;
  const anomaliesCount = processedProfiles.filter(p => p.status === LeadStatus.ANOMALO || p.status === LeadStatus.REJECTED).length;

  const ticketMedio = 1500;
  const conversionRate = 0.05;
  // Interessi calcolati sui Power Leads (liquiditÃ  immediata) + conversione standard sui qualificati
  const interessiMaturati = Math.round((powerLeadsCount * 0.20 + qualifiedLeads.length * conversionRate) * ticketMedio);

  // 4. APPLICAZIONE FILTRO INTERATTIVO (UI State)
  const filteredProfiles = processedProfiles.filter(p => {
    if (currentFilter === "POWER") return p._metrics.isPowerLead;
    if (currentFilter === "URGENT") return p._metrics.isUrgent;
    if (currentFilter === "PENDING") return p.status === LeadStatus.PENDING_OTP;
    if (currentFilter === "ANOMALIES") return p.status === LeadStatus.ANOMALO || p.status === LeadStatus.REJECTED;
    return true; // "ALL"
  });

  return (
    <div className="animate-in fade-in duration-500 space-y-8 font-sans">
      
      {/* HEADER */}
      <div className="flex justify-between items-end border-b border-slate-200 pb-4">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold text-slate-900 mb-1">{tenant.companyName || "Founders Edition"}</h2>
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-[10px] tracking-widest uppercase font-bold">Motorized Analytics & RLS Engine</p>
        </div>
        
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          {urgentCount > 0 && (
            <div className="px-3 py-1 bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 text-emerald-600 text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider font-bold rounded-full">
              {urgentCount} A Alta Urgenza
            </div>
          )}
          {powerLeadsCount > 0 && (
            <div className="px-3 py-1 bg-indigo-50 border border-indigo-200 text-indigo-600 text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider font-bold rounded-full">
              {powerLeadsCount} Power Leads
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* API KEY SECURE BOX (server safe) */}
      <div className="bg-zinc-950 rounded-xl p-6 border border-zinc-800 flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between shadow-lg gap-4 text-zinc-100">
        <div>
          <p className="text-zinc-400 text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest font-bold mb-1">Infrastruttura di Ingestion</p>
          <p className="text-zinc-400 text-sm">Header di autenticazione API: <code className="text-emerald-400 bg-zinc-900 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-zinc-800">x-rls-api-key</code></p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center">
          <input 
            type="text" 
            readOnly 
            value={tenant.apiKey} 
            className="bg-zinc-900 text-emerald-400 font-mono text-sm px-4 py-2 rounded-lg border border-zinc-800 w-full md:w-80 focus:outline-none"
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* KPI METRICS (MATEMATICA & FINANZA) */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-4 gap-6">
        <KpiBox label="Power Leads (ROI Matematico)" value={powerLeadsCount.toString()} highlight />
        <KpiBox label="LiquiditÃ  Stimata (Pipeline)" value={`â‚¬ ${interessiMaturati.toLocaleString('it-IT')}`} />
        <KpiBox label="Frizione Attiva (In OTP)" value={pendingCount.toString()} />
        <KpiBox label="Scarti & Anomalie" value={anomaliesCount.toString()} isAlert />
      </div>

      {/* FILTRI INTERATTIVI (UI TAB SWITCHER) */}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 border-b border-slate-200 pb-4">
        <FilterTab label="Tutti i Contatti" value="ALL" current={currentFilter} count={processedProfiles.length} />
        <FilterTab label="Power Leads (Alta PrioritÃ )" value="POWER" current={currentFilter} count={powerLeadsCount} />
        <FilterTab label="Urgenti (Alta Urgenza)" value="URGENT" current={currentFilter} count={urgentCount} />
        <FilterTab label="In Attesa OTP" value="PENDING" current={currentFilter} count={pendingCount} />
        <FilterTab label="Anomalie / Scarti" value="ANOMALIES" current={currentFilter} count={anomaliesCount} />
      </div>

      {/* TABELLA PRIORITARIA DINAMICA */}
      <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 shadow-sm rounded-xl overflow-hidden">
        <div className="p-5 border-b border-slate-100 bg-slate-50/50 flex justify-between items-center">
          <h3 className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 uppercase tracking-widest">
            Flusso Filtrato ({filteredProfiles.length} record visualizzati)
          </h3>
          <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider font-semibold">Algoritmo ICP Attivo</span>
        </div>
        <PriorityList leads={filteredProfiles} />
      </div>

    </div>
  );
}

function KpiBox({ label, value, highlight = false, isAlert = false }: { label: string, value: string, highlight?: boolean, isAlert?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <div className={`p-6 rounded-xl border shadow-sm transition-all ${highlight ? 'border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50' : isAlert ? 'border-red-200 bg-red-50' : 'border-slate-200 bg-white'}`}>
      <p className={`text-[10px] uppercase font-bold tracking-widest mb-1.5 ${highlight ? 'text-emerald-600' : isAlert ? 'text-red-500' : 'text-slate-500'}`}>{label}</p>
      <p className={`text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight ${highlight ? 'text-emerald-600' : isAlert ? 'text-red-600' : 'text-slate-900'}`}>{value}</p>
    </div>
  );
}

function FilterTab({ label, value, current, count }: { label: string, value: string, current: string, count: number }) {
  const isActive = current === value;
  return (
    <a 
      href={`/dashboard?filter=${value}`}
      className={`px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold tracking-wide transition-all border flex items-center space-x-2 ${
        isActive 
          ? 'bg-slate-900 text-white border-slate-900 shadow-sm' 
          : 'bg-white text-slate-600 border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50'
      }`}
    >
      <span>{label}</span>
      <span className={`px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] ${isActive ? 'bg-slate-800 text-slate-200' : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600'}`}>
        {count}
      </span>
    </a>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";

export default function PriorityList({ leads }: { leads: any[] }) {
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<"ALL" | "ACTIVE" | "REVIEW">("ALL");

  const segmentWeight: Record<string, number> = { "Urgente": 5, "Caldo": 4, "Tiepido": 3, "Freddo": 2, "Dormiente": 1 };

  const sortedLeads = [...leads].sort((a: any, b: any) => {
    if (a.status === "ACTIVE" && b.status !== "ACTIVE") return -1;
    if (b.status === "ACTIVE" && a.status !== "ACTIVE") return 1;
    if (a.status === "ACTIVE" && b.status === "ACTIVE") {
      const weightA = segmentWeight[a.segment] || 0;
      const weightB = segmentWeight[b.segment] || 0;
      if (weightA !== weightB) return weightB - weightA;
    }
    return new Date(b.lastActivityAt).getTime() - new Date(a.lastActivityAt).getTime();
  });

  const filteredLeads = sortedLeads.filter((lead: any) => {
    if (filter === "ACTIVE") return lead.status === "ACTIVE";
    if (filter === "REVIEW") return lead.status === "ANOMALO" || lead.status === "DA_VERIFICARE_MANUALMENTE";
    return true;
  });

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="flex gap-2 p-4 border-b border-slate-100 bg-white">
        <FilterBtn label="Tutti" active={filter === "ALL"} onClick={() => setFilter("ALL")} />
        <FilterBtn label="CRM Attivo" active={filter === "ACTIVE"} onClick={() => setFilter("ACTIVE")} />
        <FilterBtn label="Coda Revisione" active={filter === "REVIEW"} onClick={() => setFilter("REVIEW")} />
      </div>

      <div className="overflow-x-auto">
        <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse">
          <thead>
            <tr className="bg-slate-50 text-[10px] uppercase font-bold tracking-widest text-slate-500 border-b border-slate-200">
              <th className="p-4">Contatto</th>
              <th className="p-4">Stato Sistema</th>
              <th className="p-4">Segmento RFM</th>
              <th className="p-4">Interazioni</th>
              <th className="p-4 text-right">Ultima AttivitÃ </th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody className="text-xs text-slate-600 divide-y divide-slate-100 bg-white">
            {filteredLeads.map((lead: any) => (
              <tr key={lead.id} className="hover:bg-slate-50 transition-colors">
                <td className="p-4">
                  <p className="font-semibold text-slate-900">{lead.email}</p>
                  <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5">{lead.phone || 'Nessun Telefono'}</p>
                </td>
                <td className="p-4"><StatusBadge status={lead.status} /></td>
                <td className="p-4">
                  {lead.status === "ACTIVE" ? <SegmentBadge segment={lead.segment} /> : <span className="text-slate-300">-</span>}
                </td>
                <td className="p-4 text-[10px] font-medium tracking-widest text-slate-500">
                  <span className="text-slate-800 font-bold mr-1">{lead.contactCount}</span> Punti
                </td>
                <td className="p-4 text-right text-[10px] font-medium tracking-widest text-slate-500">
                  {new Date(lead.lastActivityAt).toLocaleDateString('it-IT', { day: '2-digit', month: 'short', hour: '2-digit', minute:'2-digit' })}
                </td>
              </tr>
            ))}
            {filteredLeads.length === 0 && (
              <tr>
                <td colSpan={5} className="p-8 text-center text-slate-400 font-medium text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest">
                  Nessun bersaglio trovato.
                </td>
              </tr>
            )}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function FilterBtn({ label, active, onClick }: { label: string, active: boolean, onClick: () => void }) {
  return (
    <button 
      onClick={onClick}
      className={`text-[10px] uppercase font-bold tracking-widest px-4 py-2 rounded-md transition-all ${active ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600' : 'bg-transparent text-slate-500 hover:bg-slate-50 hover:text-slate-700'}`}
    >
      {label}
    </button>
  );
}

function StatusBadge({ status }: { status: string }) {
  const styles: Record<string, string> = {
    "ACTIVE": "bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600 border border-emerald-200",
    "PENDING_OTP": "bg-slate-100 text-slate-500 border border-slate-200",
    "DA_VERIFICARE_MANUALMENTE": "bg-amber-50 text-amber-600 border border-amber-200",
    "ANOMALO": "bg-red-50 text-red-600 border border-red-200",
    "REJECTED": "bg-red-100 text-red-700 border border-red-300 font-bold",
  };
  return (
    <span className={`text-[9px] uppercase font-bold tracking-widest px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md ${styles[status] || styles["PENDING_OTP"]}`}>
      {status.replace(/_/g, ' ')}
    </span>
  );
}

function SegmentBadge({ segment }: { segment: string }) {
  const styles: Record<string, string> = {
    "Urgente": "text-emerald-700 bg-emerald-100 font-bold",
    "Caldo": "text-blue-700 bg-blue-100 font-semibold",
    "Tiepido": "text-amber-700 bg-amber-100 font-medium",
    "Freddo": "text-slate-600 bg-slate-100",
    "Dormiente": "text-slate-400 bg-transparent border border-slate-200",
  };
  return (
    <span className={`text-[9px] uppercase tracking-widest px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md ${styles[segment] || styles["Dormiente"]}`}>
      {segment}
    </span>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { syncRfmDatabase } from "./actions";

export default function RfmSyncButton({ tenantId }: { tenantId: string }) {
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<string | null>(null);

  async function handleSync() {
    setLoading(true);
    setStatus("Sincronizzazione RFM in corso...");
    
    try {
      const result = await syncRfmDatabase(tenantId);
      
      if (result.error) {
        setStatus(`âŒ Errore: ${result.error}`);
      } else {
        setStatus(`âœ… Allineati ${result.updatedCount} profili.`);
        setTimeout(() => setStatus(null), 3000);
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      setStatus(`âŒ Fallimento imprevisto: ${err.message}`);
    }
    
    setLoading(false);
  }

  return (
    <div className="mt-4 border-t border-gray-900 pt-4">
      <button 
        onClick={handleSync} 
        disabled={loading}
        className="w-full bg-transparent border border-gray-700 text-gray-300 font-medium text-xs tracking-widest uppercase py-3 hover:bg-white hover:text-black transition-all disabled:opacity-50"
      >
        {loading ? "Calcolo Matematico..." : "Sincronizza Database RFM"}
      </button>
      {status && <p className="mt-2 text-[10px] tracking-widest uppercase text-emerald-500 text-center">{status}</p>}
    </div>
  );
}
