"use client";

import { useState } from "react";

export default function TriggerButton() {
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<string | null>(null);

  async function fireHitlGeneration() {
    setLoading(true);
    setStatus("ðŸ§  DeepSeek in elaborazione...");
    
    try {
      // Chiamiamo il trigger che innesca il worker (che ora fa SOLO generazione, non invio)
      const res = await fetch("/api/cron/daily-fire", {
        method: "GET",
        headers: { "Authorization": `Bearer ${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_CRON_SECRET || "Al3s6i024@"}` }
      });
      
      const data = await res.json();
      
      if (data.success) {
        setStatus(`âœ… ${data.message}`); 
        setTimeout(() => setStatus(null), 5000);
      } else {
        setStatus(`âŒ Errore: ${data.error || data.message}`);
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      setStatus(`âŒ Fallimento rete: ${err.message}`);
    }
    setLoading(false);
  }

  return (
    <div className="mt-8 border-t border-gray-900 pt-8">
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
        <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-white uppercase tracking-[0.15em]">Motore IA (Generazione Bozze)</h3>
      </div>
      <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 mb-4 tracking-widest uppercase">
        Questa azione NON invia email. Interroga l'IA per generare bozze da revisionare manualmente.
      </p>
      
      <button 
        onClick={fireHitlGeneration} 
        disabled={loading}
        className="w-full bg-emerald-600/10 border border-emerald-500 text-emerald-500 font-bold text-[10px] tracking-widest uppercase py-4 hover:bg-emerald-600 hover:text-white transition-all disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(16,185,129,0.1)] hover:shadow-[0_0_20px_rgba(16,185,129,0.3)]"
      >
        {loading ? "INIEZIONE CODE..." : "GENERA BOZZE DA REVISIONARE"}
      </button>
      
      {status && (
        <p className={`mt-3 text-[10px] tracking-widest uppercase text-center ${status.includes('âŒ') ? 'text-red-500' : 'text-emerald-500'}`}>
          {status}
        </p>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";

export default async function WarmupWidget() {
  const accounts = await prisma.gmailSender.findMany();

  return (
    <div className="bg-white border border-gray-100 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-[0_8px_30px_rgb(0,0,0,0.04)]">
      <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-900 tracking-wide uppercase mb-4">
        Multi-Sling Engine
      </h3>
      
      {accounts.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">Nessun account Gmail configurato.</p>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          {accounts.map((acc) => {
            const progress = (acc.sentToday / acc.dailyLimit) * 100;
            const isWarming = acc.isActive && !acc.quarantineUntil;

            return (
              <div key={acc.id} className="flex flex-col gap-2 p-4 bg-gray-50 rounded-xl">
                <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
                  <span className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-900">{acc.email}</span>
                  <span className={`text-xs font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded-full ${isWarming ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-700' : 'bg-red-100 text-red-700'}`}>
                    {isWarming ? 'ATTIVO' : 'QUARANTENA'}
                  </span>
                </div>
                
                <div className="w-full bg-gray-200 rounded-full h-1.5 mt-2">
                  <div 
                    className="bg-black h-1.5 rounded-full transition-all duration-500" 
                    style={{ width: `${progress}%` }}
                  ></div>
                </div>
                
                <div className="flex justify-between items-center mt-1">
                  <span className="text-xs text-gray-500">CapacitÃ  odierna</span>
                  <span className="text-xs font-medium text-gray-900">
                    {acc.sentToday} / {acc.dailyLimit} inviate
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: C:\Users\Alessio\Desktop\PROGETTI\nexus-engine\src\app\dashboard\database\page.tsx

import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import LeadManager from "@/app/dashboard/LeadManager";
import TriggerButton from "@/app/dashboard/TriggerButton";
import RfmSyncButton from "@/app/dashboard/RfmSyncButton";

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

export default async function DatabasePage() {
  const tenant = await prisma.tenant.findFirst();
  if (!tenant) return null;

  const totalContacts = await prisma.profile.count({ where: { tenantId: tenant.id } });
  
  const segmentsData = await prisma.profile.groupBy({
    by: ['segment'],
    where: { tenantId: tenant.id },
    _count: { segment: true }
  });
  
  const getSegmentCount = (segName: string) => segmentsData.find(s => s.segment === segName)?._count.segment || 0;
  const segments = {
    Urgente: getSegmentCount("Urgente"),
    Caldo: getSegmentCount("Caldo"),
    Tiepido: getSegmentCount("Tiepido"),
    Freddo: getSegmentCount("Freddo"),
    Dormiente: getSegmentCount("Dormiente"),
  };

  return (
    <div className="animate-in fade-in duration-500 space-y-8">
      
      <div className="flex justify-between items-end border-b border-slate-200 pb-4">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold text-slate-900 mb-1">Database & CRM Segmentato</h2>
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-[10px] tracking-widest uppercase font-bold">Segmentazione Algoritmica e Controllo Dati</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
        {/* Distribuzione RFM */}
        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-8 shadow-sm">
          <h3 className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 mb-8 uppercase tracking-[0.15em]">Distribuzione RFM Globale</h3>
          <div className="space-y-6">
            <SegmentRow label="Urgente (>101 pt)" count={segments.Urgente} total={totalContacts} color="bg-emerald-500" />
            <SegmentRow label="Caldo (61-100 pt)" count={segments.Caldo} total={totalContacts} color="bg-blue-500" />
            <SegmentRow label="Tiepido (31-60 pt)" count={segments.Tiepido} total={totalContacts} color="bg-amber-500" />
            <SegmentRow label="Freddo (1-30 pt)" count={segments.Freddo} total={totalContacts} color="bg-slate-400" />
            <SegmentRow label="Dormiente (0 pt)" count={segments.Dormiente} total={totalContacts} color="bg-slate-200" />
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Manager Inserimento */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-1 h-full">
          <LeadManager tenantId={tenant.id} />
        </div>

        {/* Operazioni di Sistema */}
        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-8 shadow-sm flex flex-col justify-end gap-6">
          <div className="flex-1">
            <h3 className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 mb-2 uppercase tracking-[0.15em]">Motore di Sincronizzazione</h3>
            <p className="text-[10px] font-medium text-slate-500 mb-6 leading-relaxed">
              Forza il ricalcolo dei punteggi di tutto il database o avvia la generazione delle bozze IA per i nuovi lead.
            </p>
          </div>
          {/* I bottoni TriggerButton e RfmSyncButton si adatteranno se li hai modificati, o puoi usare la versione base chiara */}
          <RfmSyncButton tenantId={tenant.id} />
          <TriggerButton />
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function SegmentRow({ label, count, total, color }: { label: string, count: number, total: number, color: string }) {
  const percentage = total > 0 ? Math.round((count / total) * 100) : 0;
  return (
    <div>
      <div className="flex justify-between text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest mb-2">
        <span className="text-slate-500">{label}</span>
        <span className="text-slate-800">{count} <span className="text-slate-400 ml-1">({percentage}%)</span></span>
      </div>
      <div className="w-full bg-slate-100 h-2 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
        <div className={`h-full rounded-full ${color}`} style={{ width: `${percentage}%` }}></div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { approveAndSendEmail, rejectDraft } from "@/app/actions/hitlActions"; 

export default function HitlCard({ log }: { log: any }) {
  const [isEditing, setIsEditing] = useState(false);
  const [editedSubject, setEditedSubject] = useState(log.subject);
  const [editedBody, setEditedBody] = useState(log.errorMessage); 
  
  const [isSending, setIsSending] = useState(false);
  const [isRejecting, setIsRejecting] = useState(false);

  const SENDER_EMAIL = "alessio@designlab-group.it"; // Aggiorna se necessario

  const handleApprove = async () => {
    setIsSending(true);
    const result = await approveAndSendEmail(log.id, editedBody, editedSubject, log.tenantId, SENDER_EMAIL);
    if (!result.success) {
      alert("âŒ Errore Invio: " + result.error);
      setIsSending(false); 
    }
  };

  const handleReject = async () => {
    setIsRejecting(true);
    const result = await rejectDraft(log.id, log.tenantId);
    if (!result.success) {
      alert("âŒ Errore Scarto: " + result.error);
      setIsRejecting(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className={`bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-6 flex flex-col justify-between shadow-sm relative transition-opacity ${isSending || isRejecting ? 'opacity-50 pointer-events-none' : ''}`}>
      
      <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-4 border-b border-slate-100 pb-4">
        <div>
          <p className="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold tracking-widest text-slate-400 mb-1">
            Target: <span className="text-slate-800 font-semibold">{log.profile?.email}</span>
          </p>
          <span className="text-[9px] bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md border border-slate-200 uppercase font-bold tracking-wider">
            Modello AI Core
          </span>
        </div>
        <button 
          onClick={() => setIsEditing(!isEditing)}
          className={`text-[9px] font-bold tracking-widest uppercase px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md transition-all ${
            isEditing 
              ? "bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 border border-slate-200" 
              : "bg-white border border-slate-200 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-50"
          }`}
        >
          {isEditing ? "Chiudi Editor" : "Modifica Testo"}
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="flex-1">
        {isEditing ? (
          <div className="space-y-4 animate-in fade-in duration-300">
            <div>
              <label className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-500 mb-1.5 block">Oggetto</label>
              <input 
                value={editedSubject}
                onChange={(e) => setEditedSubject(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-slate-900 p-3 text-sm rounded-md focus:border-emerald-500 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-emerald-500 focus:outline-none transition-all"
              />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-500 mb-1.5 block">Sorgente HTML / Copy</label>
              <textarea 
                value={editedBody}
                onChange={(e) => setEditedBody(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-slate-700 p-3 text-xs h-56 rounded-md custom-scrollbar focus:border-emerald-500 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-emerald-500 focus:outline-none transition-all"
              />
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="animate-in fade-in duration-300">
            <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900 mb-4 leading-relaxed">
              Subj: {editedSubject}
            </h3>
            <p className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-400 mb-2">Live Preview</p>
            <div className="bg-white rounded-md overflow-hidden h-64 border border-slate-200 shadow-inner">
              <iframe 
                srcDoc={editedBody} 
                className="w-full h-full border-0" 
                title="Email Preview"
              />
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="flex gap-3 mt-6 border-t border-slate-100 pt-5">
        <button 
          onClick={handleApprove}
          disabled={isSending || isRejecting}
          className="flex-1 bg-emerald-500 text-white text-[10px] font-bold tracking-widest uppercase py-3 rounded-md hover:bg-emerald-600 transition-colors shadow-sm shadow-emerald-500/20 disabled:bg-slate-300 disabled:shadow-none"
        >
          {isSending ? "Iniezione Payload..." : (isEditing ? "Salva & Fuoco" : "Approva & Fuoco")}
        </button>
        <button 
          onClick={handleReject}
          disabled={isSending || isRejecting}
          className="px-6 border border-slate-200 bg-white text-slate-500 text-[10px] font-bold tracking-widest uppercase rounded-md hover:text-red-600 hover:border-red-200 hover:bg-red-50 transition-all disabled:opacity-50"
        >
          {isRejecting ? "Epurazione..." : "Scarta"}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default async function WarmupPage() {

  return (
    <div className="animate-in fade-in duration-500 space-y-8">
      
      <div className="flex justify-between items-end border-b border-slate-200 pb-4">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold text-slate-900 mb-1">Multi-Sling Radar</h2>
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-[10px] tracking-widest uppercase font-bold">Infrastruttura di Invio e Warmup</p>
        </div>
        <AddWarmupModal />
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
        {senders.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="col-span-full py-24 border border-dashed border-slate-300 bg-white rounded-xl flex flex-col items-center justify-center">

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";

export default function OtpVerification({ profileId, onVerified }: { profileId: string, onVerified?: () => void }) {
  const [token, setToken] = useState("");
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
  const [errorMsg, setErrorMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const router = useRouter();

  const handleVerify = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setIsLoading(true);
    setErrorMsg(null);

    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/leads/verify-otp", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ profileId, token })
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!data.success) {
        setErrorMsg(data.message);
      } else {
        if (onVerified) onVerified();
        else router.push("/success");
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setErrorMsg("Errore di connessione al server.");
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-8 max-w-md w-full mx-auto shadow-lg shadow-slate-200/50">
      <div className="mb-8 text-center">
        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 mb-2">Verifica IdentitÃ </h2>
        <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-medium">
          Inserisci il codice a 6 cifre ricevuto per sbloccare l'accesso.
        </p>
      </div>

      <form onSubmit={handleVerify} className="space-y-6">
        <div>
          <input 
            type="text" maxLength={6} required value={token}
            onChange={(e) => setToken(e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, ''))}
            className="w-full bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-slate-900 text-center text-3xl font-bold tracking-[0.5em] px-4 py-4 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500/20 transition-all"
            placeholder="â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢"
          />
        </div>

        {errorMsg && (
          <div className="p-3 border border-red-200 bg-red-50 text-[10px] text-red-600 uppercase font-bold tracking-widest text-center rounded-md">
            {errorMsg}
          </div>
        )}

        <button 
          type="submit" disabled={isLoading || token.length !== 6}
          className="w-full bg-emerald-500 text-white text-[11px] font-bold tracking-widest uppercase py-4 rounded-lg hover:bg-emerald-600 transition-colors disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed shadow-md shadow-emerald-500/20"
        >
          {isLoading ? "Validazione..." : "Conferma Accesso"}
        </button>
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import React, { useState } from "react";
import { Loader2, ShieldCheck, CheckCircle, AlertCircle } from "lucide-react";

type Step = 1 | 2 | 3; // 1: Raccolta Dati, 2: Verifica OTP, 3: Successo

export default function RLSLeadForm() {
  const [step, setStep] = useState<Step>(1);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [profileId, setProfileId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Stato form Step 1
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
    firstName: "",
    email: "",
    phone: "",
    urgency: "MEDIUM",
    role: "MANAGER",
    budget: "",
  });

  // Stato form Step 2
  const [otpToken, setOtpToken] = useState("");

  const handleInputChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement | HTMLSelectElement>) => {
    setFormData({ ...formData, [e.target.name]: e.target.value });
  };

  const submitLead = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setIsLoading(true);
    setError(null);

    try {
      const payload = {
        ...formData,
        budget: formData.budget ? parseFloat(formData.budget) : null,
      };

      const res = await fetch("/api/leads/ingest", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "application/json",
          // MOCK API KEY per il testing lato client (sostituire con gestione server-side sicura se necessario)
          "x-rls-api-key": "test_api_key_tenant", 
        },
        body: JSON.stringify(payload),
      });

      const data = await res.json();

      if (!res.ok) {
        throw new Error(data.message || "Errore durante l'invio dei dati.");
      }

      if (data.status === "PENDING_OTP" || data.status === "PENDING_OTP") {
        setProfileId(data.profileId);
        setStep(2);
      } else {
        // Se non raggiunge la soglia e viene scartato silenziosamente (NURTURING)
        setStep(3); 
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.message);
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const submitOtp = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setIsLoading(true);
    setError(null);

    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/leads/verify", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ profileId, token: otpToken }),
      });

      const data = await res.json();

      if (!res.ok) {
        throw new Error(data.message || "OTP non valido.");
      }

      setStep(3);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.message);
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="w-full max-w-md mx-auto bg-zinc-950 border border-zinc-800 rounded-xl shadow-2xl overflow-hidden text-zinc-100 font-sans">
      <div className="p-8">
        
        {/* HEADER */}
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center space-y-3 mb-8 text-center">
          <div className="h-12 w-12 rounded-full bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 flex items-center justify-center">
            {step === 3 ? (
              <CheckCircle className="w-6 h-6 text-green-500" />
            ) : (
              <ShieldCheck className="w-6 h-6 text-zinc-100" />
            )}
          </div>
          <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold tracking-tight">
            {step === 1 && "Verifica Accesso Sistema"}
            {step === 2 && "Autenticazione Richiesta"}
            {step === 3 && "Accesso Autorizzato"}
          </h2>
          <p className="text-sm text-zinc-500">
            {step === 1 && "Inserisci i dati operativi per procedere."}
            {step === 2 && "Abbiamo inviato un codice di sicurezza al tuo contatto."}
            {step === 3 && "IdentitÃ  verificata, sarai contattato a breve."}
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* ERROR BANNER */}
        {error && (
          <div className="mb-6 p-4 bg-red-950/30 border border-red-900/50 rounded-lg flex items-center space-x-3 text-red-400 text-sm">
            <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
            <span>{error}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* STEP 1: INGESTION FORM */}
        {step === 1 && (
          <form onSubmit={submitLead} className="space-y-4">
            <div className="space-y-4">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-zinc-400 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">Nome Completo</label>
                <input required type="text" name="firstName" value={formData.firstName} onChange={handleInputChange} className="w-full bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-lg px-4 py-3 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-white transition-all" placeholder="Es. Mario Rossi" />
              </div>
              
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-zinc-400 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">Email Aziendale</label>
                <input required type="email" name="email" value={formData.email} onChange={handleInputChange} className="w-full bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-lg px-4 py-3 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-white transition-all" placeholder="nome@azienda.it" />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-zinc-400 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">Telefono (per OTP)</label>
                <input required type="tel" name="phone" value={formData.phone} onChange={handleInputChange} className="w-full bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-lg px-4 py-3 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-white transition-all" placeholder="+39 333 0000000" />
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">

                    <option value="OWNER">Titolare / Founder</option>
                    <option value="C_LEVEL">C-Level (CEO, CTO)</option>
                    <option value="DIRECTOR">Direttore</option>
                    <option value="MANAGER">Manager</option>
                    <option value="EMPLOYEE">Dipendente</option>
                  </select>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-zinc-400 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">Urgenza</label>
                  <select name="urgency" value={formData.urgency} onChange={handleInputChange} className="w-full bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-lg px-4 py-3 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-white transition-all appearance-none">
                    <option value="HIGH">Alta (Entro 30 gg)</option>
                    <option value="MEDIUM">Media (3-6 mesi)</option>
                    <option value="LOW">Bassa (Ricerca)</option>
                  </select>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-zinc-400 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">Budget Stimato (â‚¬)</label>
                <input type="number" name="budget" value={formData.budget} onChange={handleInputChange} className="w-full bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-lg px-4 py-3 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-white transition-all" placeholder="Es. 5000" />
              </div>
            </div>

            <button type="submit" disabled={isLoading} className="w-full mt-6 bg-white text-black font-semibold rounded-lg px-4 py-3 text-sm hover:bg-zinc-200 transition-colors flex items-center justify-center disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed">
              {isLoading ? <Loader2 className="w-5 h-5 animate-spin" /> : "Procedi all'Autenticazione"}
            </button>
          </form>
        )}

        {/* STEP 2: OTP FORM */}
        {step === 2 && (
          <form onSubmit={submitOtp} className="space-y-6">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-center text-xs font-medium text-zinc-400 uppercase tracking-wider mb-3">Codice a 6 cifre</label>
              <input 
                required 
                type="text" 
                maxLength={6} 
                pattern="\d{6}"
                value={otpToken} 
                onChange={(e) => setOtpToken(e.target.value)} 
                className="w-full bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-lg px-4 py-4 text-center text-2xl tracking-[0.5em] font-mono focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-white transition-all placeholder:text-zinc-700" 
                placeholder="000000" 
              />
            </div>
            
            <button type="submit" disabled={isLoading || otpToken.length !== 6} className="w-full bg-white text-black font-semibold rounded-lg px-4 py-3 text-sm hover:bg-zinc-200 transition-colors flex items-center justify-center disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed">
              {isLoading ? <Loader2 className="w-5 h-5 animate-spin" /> : "Conferma IdentitÃ "}

FILE: C:\Users\Alessio\Desktop\PROGETTI\nexus-engine\src\lib\prisma.ts

import { PrismaClient } from '@prisma/client';
import { Pool } from 'pg';
import { PrismaPg } from '@prisma/adapter-pg';

const globalForPrisma = global as unknown as { prisma: PrismaClient };

const createPrismaClient = () => {
  let dbUrl = process.env.DATABASE_URL;

  if (!dbUrl) {
    throw new Error("[Nexus Engine] DATABASE_URL mancante nel file .env.");
  }

  // ðŸ›¡ï¸ Mantenimento crittografia militare forzata
  if (dbUrl.includes('sslmode=require')) {
    dbUrl = dbUrl.replace('sslmode=require', 'sslmode=verify-full');
  } else if (!dbUrl.includes('sslmode=')) {
    const separator = dbUrl.includes('?') ? '&' : '?';
    dbUrl = `${dbUrl}${separator}sslmode=verify-full`;
  }

  // Inizializzazione Pool e Driver Adapter per Prisma 7+
  const pool = new Pool({ connectionString: dbUrl });
  const adapter = new PrismaPg(pool);

  return new PrismaClient({
    adapter, // Connessione diretta iniettata
    log: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'development' ? ['error', 'warn'] : ['error'],
  });
};

export const prisma = globalForPrisma.prisma || createPrismaClient();

if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== 'production') globalForPrisma.prisma = prisma;

import { Queue } from 'bullmq';
import IORedis from 'ioredis';
import { prisma } from '../prisma';

// 1. Connessione Corazzata a Redis Cloud (Upstash)
// Aggiungiamo il blocco TLS per evitare l'ECONNRESET su Windows/Node24
const connection = new IORedis(process.env.REDIS_URL as string, {
  maxRetriesPerRequest: null,
  tls: {
    // ðŸ›¡ï¸ Patch vitale: ignora la validazione dei certificati locali 
    // spesso mancanti o corrotti negli store di Windows.
    rejectUnauthorized: false 
  }
});

// 2. Inizializzazione della Coda Principale
export const emailQueue = new Queue('email-queue', { 
  connection 
});

// 3. Servizio di Iniezione (Il Caricatore)
export class QueueService {
  /**
   * Seleziona chirurgicamente i lead a "Freddo" e li spara nella coda 
   */
  static async enqueueWarmupBatch(tenantId: string, campaignId: string) {
    
    const profiles = await prisma.profile.findMany({
      where: {
        tenantId: tenantId,
        segment: "Freddo",
        contactCount: 0,
      },
      take: 25, 
    });

    if (profiles.length === 0) {
      console.log(`[QueueService] Nessun bersaglio utile trovato.`);
      return 0;
    }

    let enqueued = 0;

    // Usiamo una Promise.all per velocizzare l'iniezione (non sequenziale)
    // Questo ridurrÃ  drasticamente il tempo di risposta dell'API Cron.
    await Promise.all(profiles.map(profile => 
      emailQueue.add('generate-and-draft', {
        profileId: profile.id,
        tenantId: tenantId,
        campaignId: campaignId
      })
    ));

    enqueued = profiles.length;
    console.log(`ðŸŽ¯ [QueueService] Iniettati ${enqueued} bersagli. DeepSeek in coda.`);
    return enqueued;
  }
}

/**
 * @file src/lib/services/deliveryService.ts
 * @description
 * Servizio di inoltro a latenza ottimizzata per i Lead Qualificati.
 * Gestisce l'invio via Email (simulato per Resend/Nodemailer) e via Webhook (API).
 */

import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";

export class DeliveryService {
  /**
   * Consegna il Lead Qualificato all'amministratore del Tenant.
   * Isolato con try/catch globale per impedire il crash del Worker chiamante.
   */
  static async sendLeadToTenant(profileId: string, tenantId: string): Promise<boolean> {
    console.log(`[DELIVERY] Avvio inoltro Lead [${profileId}] al Tenant [${tenantId}]...`);

    try {
      // 1. Estrazione dati relazionali (Profile + Tenant)
      const profile = await prisma.profile.findUnique({
        where: { id: profileId },
      });

      const tenant = await prisma.tenant.findUnique({
        where: { id: tenantId },
      });

      if (!profile || !tenant) {
        console.error(`âŒ [DELIVERY] Dati mancanti. Inoltro annullato.`);
        return false;
      }

      if (!tenant.adminEmail) {
        console.warn(`âš ï¸ [DELIVERY] Nessuna adminEmail configurata per il Tenant ${tenant.companyName}.`);
        // In un caso reale, potremmo comunque tentare il webhook
      }

      // 2. Costruzione Payload Standardizzato
      const leadPayload = {
        lead_id: profile.id,
        contact: {
          first_name: profile.firstName,
          last_name: profile.lastName,
          email: profile.email,
          phone: profile.phone,
        },
        qualification: {
          icp_score: profile.icpScore,
          urgency: profile.urgency,
          role: profile.role,
          budget: profile.budget,
          problem_description: profile.problemDescription,
        },
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      };

      // 3. Esecuzione Parallela dei Canali di Consegna
      const deliveryTasks = [];

      if (tenant.adminEmail) {
        deliveryTasks.push(this.simulateEmailDelivery(tenant.adminEmail, leadPayload, tenant.companyName));
      }

      // Simulazione presenza Webhook nel record Tenant (es. tenant.webhookUrl)
      // Ipotizziamo una variabile fittizia per dimostrazione
      const webhookUrl = process.env.MOCK_WEBHOOK_URL; 
      if (webhookUrl) {
        deliveryTasks.push(this.fireWebhook(webhookUrl, leadPayload));
      }

      // Attendiamo l'esito di tutti i canali senza bloccarci al primo errore
      const results = await Promise.allSettled(deliveryTasks);
      
      const hasSuccess = results.some((result) => result.status === "fulfilled" && result.value === true);
      
      if (hasSuccess) {
        console.log(`âœ… [DELIVERY] Lead [${profileId}] consegnato con successo.`);
        return true;
      } else {
        console.error(`âŒ [DELIVERY] Fallimento totale su tutti i canali di inoltro per Lead [${profileId}].`);
        return false;
      }

    } catch (error: unknown) {
      const errorMessage = error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Errore sconosciuto";
      console.error(`ðŸš¨ [DELIVERY FATAL ERROR]: ${errorMessage}`);
      return false; // Il worker BullMQ riproverÃ  in base alla policy di backoff
    }
  }

  /**
   * Simulazione invio Email tramite provider (es. Resend)
   */
  private static async simulateEmailDelivery(to: string, payload: any, companyName: string): Promise<boolean> {
    try {
      const emailText = `
ðŸš€ NUOVO LEAD QUALIFICATO PER ${companyName} ðŸš€

Il sistema RLS ha setacciato e qualificato un nuovo prospect.

DATI CONTATTO:
- Nome: ${payload.contact.first_name || "N/D"} ${payload.contact.last_name || "N/D"}
- Email: ${payload.contact.email}
- Telefono: ${payload.contact.phone || "N/D"}

PROFILAZIONE (ICP Score: ${payload.qualification.icp_score}/100):
- Urgenza: ${payload.qualification.urgency}
- Ruolo: ${payload.qualification.role}
- Budget: â‚¬${payload.qualification.budget || "Non specificato"}

PROBLEMA: 
${payload.qualification.problem_description || "N/D"}

Accedi alla dashboard per i dettagli completi.
      `;

      // Simula il ritardo di rete API (es. chiamata a Resend)
      await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 800));
      
      // In produzione: await resend.emails.send({ ... })
      console.log(`âœ‰ï¸ [EMAIL] Inviata a ${to}:\n${emailText}`);
      
      return true;
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`âŒ [EMAIL] Errore invio a ${to}`, error);
      return false;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Inoltro payload tramite Webhook con timeout di sicurezza (5 secondi)
   */
  private static async fireWebhook(url: string, payload: any): Promise<boolean> {
    try {
      // Usa AbortSignal per prevenire code bloccate da CRM lenti
      const controller = new AbortController();
      const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 5000);

      const response = await fetch(url, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "application/json",
          "User-Agent": "RLS-Delivery-Engine/1.0",
        },
        body: JSON.stringify(payload),
        signal: controller.signal,
      });

      clearTimeout(timeoutId);

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(`HTTP Status ${response.status}`);
      }

      console.log(`ðŸ”Œ [WEBHOOK] Payload consegnato a ${url} (Status: ${response.status})`);
      return true;
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      const errorMessage = error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Errore timeout/rete";
      console.error(`âŒ [WEBHOOK] Fallimento consegna a ${url}: ${errorMessage}`);
      return false;
    }
  }
}

import OpenAI from "openai";

export interface IntentData {
  hasIntent: boolean;
  topic?: string;
  confidenceScore?: number;
}

export interface GhostEmailContent {
  subject: string;
  email_body: string;
}

export class GhostService {
  private static aiClient = new OpenAI({
    apiKey: process.env.DEEPSEEK_API_KEY,
    baseURL: "https://api.deepseek.com",
  });

  public static async analyzeIntentGraph(email: string): Promise<IntentData> {
    if (!email) return { hasIntent: false };

    const hash = email.split("").reduce((acc, char) => acc + char.charCodeAt(0), 0);
    const confidenceScore = (hash % 100) / 100; 
    
    // Topic riallineati al target "Paolucci Advisory"
    const intentTopics = [
      "come aumentare i margini senza nuovi clienti",
      "calcolo gap di fatturato aziendale",
      "riattivazione database clienti b2b",
      "consulenza direzionale aumento profitti",
      "ottimizzazione costi acquisizione clienti"
    ];
    const detectedTopic = intentTopics[hash % intentTopics.length];

    if (confidenceScore > 0.75) {
      return {
        hasIntent: true,
        topic: detectedTopic,
        confidenceScore
      };
    }

    return { hasIntent: false };
  }

  public static async generateContextualEmail(
    nome: string, 
    intentTopic: string, 
    brandVoice: string
  ): Promise<GhostEmailContent> {
    
    const systemPrompt = `Sei un Elite Copywriter specializzato in riattivazione B2B.
Business: ${brandVoice || "Paolucci Advisory"}.
Destinatario: ${nome || "Imprenditore"}.

DATI DI INTENTO (SEGRETI):
Cerca soluzioni su: "${intentTopic}".

IL TUO COMPITO:
Aggancialo. NON svelare che lo stiamo tracciando. 

REGOLE DI SCRITTURA (TASSATIVE):
1. Paragrafi di massimo 1-2 frasi. 
2. Usa "Bucket Brigades" (es. "Il problema Ã¨ questo:", "La veritÃ :").
3. Usa il tag <strong> sulle parole chiave per lo skimming.
4. Tono: Da imprenditore a imprenditore. Nessuna fuffa. Autorevole e analitico.
5. Recency: Insight finale alla fine assoluta del testo.

FORMATO OUTPUT (SOLO JSON):
{
  "subject": "Oggetto atomico (max 6 parole)",
  "email_body": "Codice HTML puro. Usa SOLO <p> e <strong>."
}`;

    try {
      const completion = await this.aiClient.chat.completions.create({
        model: "deepseek-chat",
        messages: [{ role: "system", content: systemPrompt }],
        temperature: 0.4, 
        response_format: { type: "json_object" }
      });

      const parsed = JSON.parse(completion.choices[0]?.message?.content ?? "{}");
      return {
        subject: parsed.subject || "Una riflessione sui tuoi margini",
        email_body: parsed.email_body
      };

    } catch (error) {
      throw new Error("Generazione email fallita.");
    }
  }
}

import nodemailer from 'nodemailer';
import { prisma } from '@/lib/prisma';

// Delay random tra 90 e 180 secondi (Anti-Spam)
const randomDelay = () => Math.floor(Math.random() * (180000 - 90000 + 1) + 90000);
const sleep = (ms: number) => new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));

export async function sendViaGmailRotator(to: string, subject: string, htmlContent: string) {
  // 1. Cerca il mittente migliore: attivo, sotto limite e non in quarantena
  const sender = await prisma.gmailSender.findFirst({
    where: {
      isActive: true,
      sentToday: { lt: prisma.gmailSender.fields.dailyLimit },
      OR: [
        { quarantineUntil: null },
        { quarantineUntil: { lt: new Date() } }
      ]
    },
    orderBy: { lastSentAt: 'asc' } // Prendi quello usato meno di recente
  });

  if (!sender) {
    throw new Error("Tutti gli account hanno superato il limite o sono in quarantena.");
  }

  console.log(`[Email Engine] Selezionato ${sender.email}. Limite attuale: ${sender.sentToday}/${sender.dailyLimit}`);

  // 2. Transporter Nodemailer (Motore Universale: Gmail + Custom SMTP)
  const transporter = nodemailer.createTransport(
    sender.provider === "GMAIL" 
      ? {
          service: 'gmail',
          auth: { user: sender.email, pass: sender.appPassword }
        }
      : {
          host: sender.smtpHost || "", // Fallback per sicurezza TypeScript
          port: sender.smtpPort || 465,
          secure: sender.smtpPort === 465, // true per 465, false per le altre porte
          auth: { user: sender.email, pass: sender.appPassword }
        }
  );

  try {
    // 3. Jitter: Aspetta come un umano prima di inviare
    const delay = randomDelay();
    console.log(`â³ [Delay] Attesa di ${delay / 1000}s per simulare comportamento umano...`);
    await sleep(delay);

    // 4. Fuoco!
    await transporter.sendMail({
      from: `"Massimiliano Paolucci" <${sender.email}>`,
      to,
      subject,
      html: htmlContent
    });

    // 5. Aggiorna DB
    await prisma.gmailSender.update({
      where: { id: sender.id },
      data: {
        sentToday: { increment: 1 },
        lastSentAt: new Date()
      }
    });

    console.log(`âœ… [Successo] Email inviata da ${sender.email}`);
    return true;

  } catch (error: any) {
    const errStr = error.message || "Errore SMTP";
    console.error(`âŒ [Errore SMTP] ${sender.email}:`, errStr);

    // Logica di Auto-Quarantena
    const tomorrow = new Date();
    tomorrow.setDate(tomorrow.getDate() + 1);

    await prisma.gmailSender.update({
      where: { id: sender.id },
      data: {
        isActive: errStr.includes('535') ? false : true, // 535 = Password errata
        quarantineUntil: errStr.includes('421') || errStr.includes('limit') ? tomorrow : null, // 421 = Rate limit
        errorLog: errStr
      }
    });

    throw new Error(`Invio fallito tramite ${sender.email}. Riprova in coda.`);
  }
}

import { ImapFlow } from 'imapflow';
import nodemailer from 'nodemailer';
import { prisma } from '@/lib/prisma';

export class P2PWarmupService {
  
  // Genera messaggi finti per ingannare i filtri
  private static getMockContent() {
    const messages = [
      { subject: "Aggiornamento meeting", body: "Ciao, confermiamo la call di domani alle 15? Ho letto il report, sembra tutto ok." },
      { subject: "Fattura Q3", body: "Potresti controllare l'allegato della fattura? Mi sembra ci sia un errore sull'imponibile." },
      { subject: "Revisione progetto", body: "Ti mando le modifiche richieste. Fammi sapere se procediamo o se serve un'altra revisione." },
      { subject: "Accesso piattaforma", body: "Non riesco a loggarmi sul CRM, mi rimandi le credenziali per favore?" }
    ];
    return messages[Math.floor(Math.random() * messages.length)];
  }

  public static async runPingPongCycle() {
    console.log("ðŸ”„ [Nexus P2P] Avvio ciclo di riscaldamento interattivo...");

    // 1. Peschiamo due account casuali e attivi dalla tua Farm
    const accounts = await prisma.gmailSender.findMany({ 
      where: { isActive: true },
      take: 2 // Ne bastano 2 per fare ping-pong
    });

    if (accounts.length < 2) {
      console.log("âš ï¸ [Nexus P2P] Servono almeno 2 account configurati per fare ping-pong.");
      return;
    }

    const sender = accounts[0];
    const receiver = accounts[1];
    const content = this.getMockContent();
    const tag = `[NX-${Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000)}]`; // Tag univoco per rintracciare la mail
    const subject = `${tag} ${content.subject}`;

    try {
      // ==========================================
      // FASE 1: L'INVIO (Sender -> Receiver)
      // ==========================================
      const transporter = nodemailer.createTransport({
        service: 'gmail',
        auth: { user: sender.email, pass: sender.appPassword }
      });

      await transporter.sendMail({
        from: sender.email,
        to: receiver.email,
        subject: subject,
        text: content.body
      });
      console.log(`ðŸ“¤ [P2P] ${sender.email} ha scritto a ${receiver.email}`);

      // Pausa di 10 secondi per dare tempo a Google di consegnare la mail
      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 10000));

      // ==========================================
      // FASE 2: LA LETTURA E IL RECUPERO DALLO SPAM
      // ==========================================
      const client = new ImapFlow({
        host: 'imap.gmail.com',
        port: 993,
        secure: true,
        auth: { user: receiver.email, pass: receiver.appPassword },
        logger: false 
      });

      await client.connect();
      
      // Prima cerchiamo nello SPAM
      try {
        let spamLock = await client.getMailboxLock('[Gmail]/Spam');
        const spamMessages = await client.search({ subject: tag });
        
        // ðŸ›¡ï¸ CORREZIONE TYPESCRIPT QUI:
        if (Array.isArray(spamMessages) && spamMessages.length > 0) {
          console.log(`ðŸ›¡ï¸ [P2P] Email trovata nello SPAM. Spostamento in Inbox in corso...`);
          await client.messageMove(spamMessages, 'INBOX');
        }
        spamLock.release();
      } catch (e) { /* Ignora cartelle non trovate */ }

      // Ora andiamo in INBOX a leggerla
      let inboxLock = await client.getMailboxLock('INBOX');
      const inboxMessages = await client.search({ subject: tag });
      
      // ðŸ›¡ï¸ CORREZIONE TYPESCRIPT QUI:
      if (Array.isArray(inboxMessages) && inboxMessages.length > 0) {
        await client.messageFlagsAdd(inboxMessages, ['\\Seen']);
        console.log(`ðŸ‘€ [P2P] ${receiver.email} ha APERTO e LETTO l'email.`);
      }
      inboxLock.release();
      await client.logout();

      // ==========================================
      // FASE 3: LA RISPOSTA (Receiver -> Sender)
      // ==========================================
      const replyTransporter = nodemailer.createTransport({
        service: 'gmail',
        auth: { user: receiver.email, pass: receiver.appPassword }
      });

      await replyTransporter.sendMail({
        from: receiver.email,
        to: sender.email,
        subject: `Re: ${subject}`,
        text: "Ricevuto, ci guardo subito e ti aggiorno. Grazie!"
      });
      console.log(`âœ… [P2P] ${receiver.email} ha RISPOSTO a ${sender.email}`);

    } catch (error) {
      console.error("âŒ [P2P Error] Fallimento nel ciclo Ping-Pong:", error);
    }
  }
}

import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";

export type RediSegment = "Freddo" | "Tiepido" | "Caldo" | "Urgente" | "Dormiente";

export class RfmService {
  // Pesi dinamici configurabili (Redi Method)
  private static WEIGHT_OPEN = 5;
  private static WEIGHT_CLICK = 10;

  /**
   * Calcolo Punteggio Attivo (Interazioni via Email/Form)
   */
  public static calculateActiveScore(contactCount: number, totalClicks: number = 0, lastActivityAt: Date | null): number {
    let score = 0;
    
    // Frequenza e ProfonditÃ 
    score += (contactCount * this.WEIGHT_OPEN);
    score += (totalClicks * this.WEIGHT_CLICK);

    // Recency (Bonus per attivitÃ  recente)
    if (lastActivityAt) {
      const diffInDays = Math.floor((Date.now() - lastActivityAt.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
      if (diffInDays <= 2) score += 15;
      else if (diffInDays <= 7) score += 10;
      else if (diffInDays <= 30) score += 5;
    }

    return score;
  }

  /**
   * Assegnazione Livello (Metodo Redi)
   */
  public static classifyRediSegment(totalScore: number): RediSegment {
    if (totalScore >= 101) return "Urgente";
    if (totalScore >= 61) return "Caldo";
    if (totalScore >= 31) return "Tiepido";
    if (totalScore > 0) return "Freddo";
    return "Dormiente"; 
  }

  /**
   * TRIGGER CENTRALE: Sincronizzazione Totale Punteggio
   */
  public static async syncProfileScore(profileId: string) {
    try {
      const profile = await prisma.profile.findUnique({
        where: { id: profileId }
      });

      if (!profile) return null;

      // 1. Estrazione Variabili (Uso 'any' temporaneo per bypassare controlli restrittivi in caso di mancato sync dello schema)
      const activeScore = this.calculateActiveScore(
        profile.contactCount, 
        (profile as any).totalClicks || 0,
        profile.lastActivityAt
      );
      
      const passiveScore = (profile as any).passiveScore || 0;
      const coherenceScore = (profile as any).coherenceScore || 0;

      // 2. Fusione Matematica
      const integratedScore = activeScore + passiveScore + coherenceScore;

      // 3. Classificazione Automatica
      const newSegment = this.classifyRediSegment(integratedScore);

      // 4. Aggiornamento Chirurgico Database
      await prisma.profile.update({
        where: { id: profileId },
        data: {
          segment: newSegment,
        }
      });

      console.log(`ðŸŽ¯ [RFM Sync] Profilo ${profile.email} riallineato: ${integratedScore} pt. -> Segmento: ${newSegment}`);

      return { integratedScore, newSegment };

    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`âŒ [RFM Sync Error] Impossibile ricalcolare score per ${profileId}:`, error);
      return null;
    }
  }
}

/**
 * @file src/lib/services/spamService.ts
 * @description
 * Motore euristico di analisi Spam. Simula le regole di SpamAssassin analizzando
 * il payload HTML dell'email prima dell'invio. 
 * Se il punteggio > 3, l'email Ã¨ rischiosa per la deliverability e viene bloccata.
 */

export class SpamService {
  public static calculateScore(subject: string, htmlBody: string): number {
    let score = 0;
    const bodyLower = htmlBody.toLowerCase();
    const subjectLower = subject.toLowerCase();

    // 1. Parole ad alto rischio Spam (Trigger Finanziari/Urgenza)
    const spamWords = ["gratis", "soldi", "guadagno", "urgente", "vincita", "offerta", "clicca qui"];
    spamWords.forEach(word => {
      if (bodyLower.includes(word)) score += 1.5;
      if (subjectLower.includes(word)) score += 2.0;
    });

    // 2. Abuso di formattazione (Maiuscole, Punteggiatura estrema)
    if ((subject.match(/[A-Z]/g)?.length || 0) > subject.length * 0.5) score += 2.0; // Oggetto 50% CAPS
    if (subject.includes('!!!') || subject.includes('???')) score += 1.5;

    // 3. Rapporto Immagini/Testo (Regola aurea della deliverability)
    const imageCount = (htmlBody.match(/<img/g) || []).length;
    const textLength = htmlBody.replace(/<[^>]*>?/gm, '').trim().length;
    
    if (imageCount > 0 && textLength < 100) {
      score += 2.5; // Molte immagini, poco testo = Filtro Promozioni garantito
    }

    // 4. Link Spam (Troppi link nel corpo disperdono la CTA)
    const linkCount = (htmlBody.match(/<a href/g) || []).length;
    if (linkCount > 3) score += 1.5;

    return parseFloat(score.toFixed(2));
  }
}

export class TrafficScoringService {
  /**
   * AREA 2: QualitÃ  delle pagine visitate
   * Decodifica l'URL e assegna il peso specifico.
   */
  public static calculatePageScore(url: string): number {
    const u = url.toLowerCase();
    
    if (u.includes('contatti')) return 15;
    if (u.includes('pricing') || u.includes('prezzi') || u.includes('preventivo')) return 12;
    if (u.includes('case-study') || u.includes('portfolio') || u.includes('risultati')) return 7;
    if (u.includes('servizi') || u.includes('prodotti')) return 5;
    if (u.includes('chi-siamo') || u.includes('about')) return 3;
    
    // Homepage fallback
    if (u.endsWith('.com/') || u.endsWith('.it/') || u === '/') return 2;
    
    return 0; // Pagine non strategiche
  }

  /**
   * AREA 1: QualitÃ  della visita (Tempo sul sito)
   * Calcola il delta bonus basato sull'incremento di durata rispetto al pulse precedente.
   */
  public static getDurationBonus(currentDuration: number, previousDuration: number): number {
    let deltaBonus = 0;
    
    // 45-90 secondi (+5)
    if (previousDuration < 45 && currentDuration >= 45) deltaBonus += 5;
    // 90-180 secondi (+10 totali, aggiungiamo il delta di 5)
    if (previousDuration < 90 && currentDuration >= 90) deltaBonus += 5;
    // > 3 minuti (+15 totali, aggiungiamo il delta di 5)
    if (previousDuration < 180 && currentDuration >= 180) deltaBonus += 5;
    
    return deltaBonus;
  }
}

import { randomInt } from "crypto";

export interface ValidationResult {
  isValid: boolean;
  isTimeout: boolean;
  error?: string;
}

export class ValidationService {
  /**
   * LIVELLO 1: Validazione Tecnica Hard (Mock per API esterne)
   * Integrare qui chiamate reali a Hunter.io, ZeroBounce o NumVerify.
   */
  public static async verifyEmailTechnical(email: string): Promise<ValidationResult> {
    try {
      // Simulazione chiamata API esterna con timeout
      if (!email.includes("@") || email.includes("test.com")) {
        return { isValid: false, isTimeout: false, error: "Dominio email non valido o usa-e-getta." };
      }
      return { isValid: true, isTimeout: false };
    } catch (error) {
      // Cattura il timeout per attivare la coda DA_VERIFICARE_MANUALMENTE
      return { isValid: false, isTimeout: true, error: "Timeout API Email" };
    }
  }

  public static async verifyPhoneTechnical(phone: string): Promise<ValidationResult> {
    try {
      if (phone.length < 9) {
        return { isValid: false, isTimeout: false, error: "Formato telefono non valido." };
      }
      return { isValid: true, isTimeout: false };
    } catch (error) {
      return { isValid: false, isTimeout: true, error: "Timeout API Telefono" };
    }
  }

  /**
   * LIVELLO 3: Scoring di Coerenza
   * Disinnesca i perditempo identificando discrepanze logiche nei dati.
   */
  public static calculateCoherence(budget?: number, industry?: string, expectedIndustry?: string): number {
    let score = 0;
    
    // Esempio logica di coerenza: Budget alto ma settore fuori target
    if (budget && budget > 10000) score += 20;
    if (!budget) score -= 10;
    
    if (industry && expectedIndustry && industry.toLowerCase() !== expectedIndustry.toLowerCase()) {
      score -= 20; // Flag ANOMALO
    } else if (industry) {
      score += 15;
    }

    return score;
  }

  /**
   * LIVELLO 2: Generazione OTP
   */
  public static generateNumericOTP(length: number = 6): string {
    const min = Math.pow(10, length - 1);
    const max = Math.pow(10, length) - 1;
    return randomInt(min, max).toString();
  }
}

/**
 * @file src/lib/services/vaultService.ts
 * @description
 * Motore di generazione testuale potenziato da Claude (Anthropic). 
 * Applica rigorosamente le logiche di conversione violenta, Egoismo Strategico, 
 * e i framework PAS/BAB per il brand Paolucci Advisory. 
 */

import Anthropic from '@anthropic-ai/sdk';

export interface GeneratedContent {
  subject: string;
  email_body: string;
}

export interface EmailGenerationParams {
  nome: string;
  angolo: string;
  isProxy?: boolean;
  brandVoice?: string;
  profileId: string;
  tenantId: string;
  destinationUrl?: string; 
  timeInactive?: string;
  segment?: string;      
}

export class VaultService {
  // Inizializzazione protetta: previene il crash se la chiave manca nel .env
  private static anthropic = new Anthropic({
    apiKey: process.env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY || "chiave_mancante_bypass", 
  });

  private static ARCH_COPY_VAULT: Record<string, string> = {
    "Cambio di Prospettiva": `Per anni ho fatto la stessa cosa che fanno tutti... [TAG_LINK]`,
    "La domanda che nessuno si fa": `Mi sono fatto una domanda [TEMPO_TRASCORSO] fa... [TAG_LINK]`,
    "La storia breve": `Ho un collega, lavora in un settore diverso... [TAG_LINK]`,
    "Riconoscimento Diretto": `Ãˆ passato [TEMPO_TRASCORSO] dall'ultima volta che ci siamo sentiti... [TAG_LINK]`,
    "Il cambio che giustifica il ritorno": `Circa [TEMPO_TRASCORSO] fa ho smesso di fare una cosa che facevo sempre... [TAG_LINK]`,
    "La domanda diretta": `Non ci sentiamo da [TEMPO_TRASCORSO]. Ha ancora senso per te ricevere le mie mail? [TAG_LINK]`,
    "Re-permission Onesta": `Sei tra i miei contatti da [TEMPO_TRASCORSO]... [TAG_LINK]`,
    "Valore prima di tutto": `Non mi presento perchÃ© ci siamo giÃ  incrociati... [TAG_LINK]`,
    "Punto di ingresso esterno": `Non ti scrivo da [TEMPO_TRASCORSO], ed ecco perchÃ© ti contatto oggi... [TAG_LINK]`
  };

  public static async generateEmailContent(params: EmailGenerationParams): Promise<GeneratedContent> {
    const { nome, angolo, profileId, tenantId, destinationUrl, timeInactive, segment } = params;
    
    const baseUrl = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL || "https://paolucciadvisory.com";
    const finalDestination = destinationUrl || "https://paolucciadvisory.com/libro-arch";
    const trackingLink = `${baseUrl}/api/track/click?uid=${profileId}&tid=${tenantId}&url=${encodeURIComponent(finalDestination)}`;

    const rawTemplate = this.ARCH_COPY_VAULT[angolo] || this.ARCH_COPY_VAULT["Cambio di Prospettiva"];
    const baseTemplate = rawTemplate.replace(/\[TEMPO_TRASCORSO\]/g, timeInactive || "molto tempo");
    
    const finalHtmlMock = `<p>Ciao ${nome || "Imprenditore"},</p><p>${baseTemplate.replace("[TAG_LINK]", `<a href="${trackingLink}" style="color: #d93025; font-weight: bold;">Accedi Ora</a>`)}</p>`;

    // SHIELD: Se non c'Ã¨ la chiave reale, usa il fallback e non chiamare l'API
    if (!process.env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY || process.env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY === "chiave_mancante_bypass") {
      console.warn("âš ï¸ [VaultService] ANTHROPIC_API_KEY mancante. Uso fallback statico.");
      return {
        subject: "I soldi fermi nel tuo database",
        email_body: finalHtmlMock
      };
    }

    const businessContext = "Paolucci Advisory";
    const productTarget = "Libro: 'Non ti servono nuovi clienti' (Up-sell implicito: Audit ARCH).";
    const userSegment = segment || "Freddo";
    const framework = (userSegment === "Freddo" || userSegment === "Dormiente") 
      ? "PAS (Problem-Agitate-Solve)" 
      : "BAB (Before-After-Bridge)";

    const systemPrompt = `CORE DIRECTIVE OVERRIDE: Sei "Massimo Paolucci", Marketing Strategist & Elite Copywriter.
Azienda: ${businessContext}. Prodotto: ${productTarget}. Destinatario: ${nome || "Imprenditore"}. Segmento RFM: ${userSegment}.

TESTO BASE:
"""
${baseTemplate}
"""

DIRETTIVA:
1. Egoismo Strategico e framework ${framework}. Disintegra obiezioni.
2. Ritmo: Paragrafi brevi. Usa <br><br>.
3. Alla fine usa ESATTAMENTE: "<a href='${trackingLink}'>Accedi Ora</a>".
Rispondi in JSON puro: { "subject": "...", "email_body": "..." }`;

    try {
      const response = await this.anthropic.messages.create({
        model: 'claude-3-haiku-20240307',
        max_tokens: 1024,
        temperature: 0.6,
        system: systemPrompt,
        messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'Genera email' }]
      });

      let rawText = response.content.find(block => block.type === 'text')?.text || "{}";
      rawText = rawText.replace(/```json/g, '').replace(/```/g, '').trim();
      
      const parsed = JSON.parse(rawText);

      return {
        subject: parsed.subject || "I soldi fermi nel tuo database",
        email_body: parsed.email_body || finalHtmlMock
      };
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("[VaultService] Errore Claude AI:", error);
      // Fallback finale in caso di timeout API
      return { subject: "Comunicazione Importante", email_body: finalHtmlMock };
    }
  }
}

import { Worker, Job } from 'bullmq';
import { prisma } from '../prisma';
import OpenAI from 'openai';
import IORedis from 'ioredis';

// Inizializzazione DeepSeek tramite SDK OpenAI
const deepseek = new OpenAI({
  baseURL: 'https://api.deepseek.com',
  apiKey: process.env.DEEPSEEK_API_KEY,
});

const redisConnection = new IORedis(process.env.REDIS_URL as string, {
  maxRetriesPerRequest: null,
  tls: {
    rejectUnauthorized: false // ðŸ›¡ï¸ Patch obbligatoria per prevenire l'ECONNRESET su Windows/Node24
  }
});

const APP_URL = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL || "http://localhost:3000";

console.log("âš™ï¸ [DeepSeek Worker] Demone di generazione testuale HITL attivo...");

const worker = new Worker('email-queue', async (job: Job) => {
  const { profileId, tenantId, campaignId } = job.data;

  try {
    const profile = await prisma.profile.findUnique({
      where: { id: profileId }
    });

    if (!profile) throw new Error(`Profilo ${profileId} inesistente.`);

    console.log(`ðŸ§  [DeepSeek Worker] Ingegnerizzazione bozza HITL per: ${profile.email}`);

    // ==========================================================
    // NEURO-PROMPT ENGINEERING HIGH-TICKET (INBOX PRIMARIA GMAIL)
    // ==========================================================
    const systemPrompt = `Sei l'Head of Growth di "Design Lab Studio". Scrivi cold email B2B per fissare un check veloce.
Regole psicologiche e di deliverability fondamentali:
1. ANNULLA i convenevoli. Vietato iniziare con "Spero stia bene", "Mi chiamo", "Sono il responsabile di". Vai dritto al punto.
2. Identifica un'inefficienza visiva o di conversione basandoti sull'angolo fornito. Tono freddo, clinico, da pari a pari (C-Level).
3. Scrivi un blocco di massimo 3-4 frasi brevi. Massimo 70 parole totali. Deve sembrare un testo inviato rapidamente da iPhone.
4. Chiudi con una sola domanda secca, binaria e a frizione zero per aprire la conversazione.
5. Restituisci SOLO il testo pulito, usa esclusivamente i tag <br><br> per andare a capo. Nessun tag html, body o blocco di codice markdown (\`\`\`).`;

    const userPrompt = ` Sotto-angolo d'attacco: ${profile.arch_angle || 'Il design debole sta bruciando i tassi di conversione delle loro landing page'}.
Destinatario: ${profile.firstName || 'il responsabile'}.
Genera il corpo del messaggio.`;

    // Chiamata API per il testo dell'email
    const completion = await deepseek.chat.completions.create({
      messages: [
        { role: "system", content: systemPrompt },
        { role: "user", content: userPrompt }
      ],
      model: "deepseek-chat",
      temperature: 0.35, // Abbassato per garantire massima stabilitÃ  e aderenza ai vincoli di sintesi
      max_tokens: 150,
    });

    let generatedHtml = completion.choices[0].message.content?.trim() || "Errore di generazione testo.";
    
    // Pulizia radicale di eventuali sbavature o blocchi markdown restituiti dall'IA
    generatedHtml = generatedHtml
      .replace(/```html/gi, '')
      .replace(/```/gi, '')
      .replace(/^"|"$/g, '') // Rimuove eventuali virgolette esterne
      .trim();

    // ðŸ’‰ INIEZIONE DEL DNA (SHADOW TRACKING LINK)
    let infectedBody = generatedHtml.replace(
      /href=["'](https?:\/\/(www\.)?[^"']*)["']/gi, 
      (match, url) => {
        const separator = url.includes('?') ? '&' : '?';
        return `href="${APP_URL}/api/tracking?url=${encodeURIComponent(url)}&uid=${profile.id}&tid=${tenantId}"`;
      }
    );

    // ðŸ›¡ï¸ WRAPPER HTML ULTRA-LIGHT (Simula Plain Text per schivare la tab Promozioni)
    const finalHtml = `
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="it">
<head><meta charset="UTF-8"></head>
<body style="font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 15px; color: #111111; line-height: 1.5; max-width: 600px; margin: 0;">
  <div>${infectedBody}</div>
  <img src="${APP_URL}/api/tracking/open?uid=${profile.id}&tid=${tenantId}" width="1" height="1" style="display:none;" alt="" />
</body>
</html>`.trim();

    // Chiamata API per l'Oggetto dell'email (Ottimizzato per tassi di apertura interni)
    const subjectCompletion = await deepseek.chat.completions.create({
      messages: [
        { role: "system", content: "Sei un analista. Scrivi un oggetto per un'email interna. Massimo 2-4 parole, tutto minuscolo, nessuna punteggiatura, zero parole di vendita. Deve sembrare una nota veloce tra colleghi." },
        { role: "user", content: `Genera l'oggetto basandoti su questa email: ${generatedHtml}` }
      ],
      model: "deepseek-chat",
      temperature: 0.4,
      max_tokens: 30
    });

    let generatedSubject = subjectCompletion.choices[0].message.content?.trim() || "quick update";
    generatedSubject = generatedSubject.replace(/['"â€œâ€]/g, '').toLowerCase();

    // ðŸ“¥ DELEGA IN QUARANTENA (HITL)
    // Salviamo lo stato come PENDING_APPROVAL. La dashboard HITL leggerÃ  questo record.
    await prisma.automationLog.create({
      data: {
        tenantId,
        profileId,
        campaignId,
        emailAddress: profile.email,
        status: "PENDING_APPROVAL", 
        subject: generatedSubject,
        errorMessage: finalHtml, // Il codice HTML tracciato Ã¨ stoccato qui dentro, pronto per la review manuale
      }
    });

    console.log(`ðŸ“¥ [HITL Queue] Bozza stoccata in attesa di approvazione per: ${profile.email}`);

  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error(`âŒ [DeepSeek Worker ERROR] Fallimento sul lead ${profileId}:`, error.message);
    throw error;
  }
}, { connection: redisConnection });

worker.on('failed', (job, err) => {
  console.log(`âš ï¸ Job ${job?.id} fallito nel cluster. Motivo: ${err.message}`);
});
